The STT inference asset is not operator-selectable. Task 4 adds the project-owned locally synthesized Windows SAPI speech fixture at `tests/fixtures/runtime_health/stt-human-en-v1.mp4` and its immutable deterministic manifest at `tests/fixtures/runtime_health/stt-human-en-v1.json`. This is not represented as a consented human recording: the manifest records the approved provenance deviation (locally synthesized, project-owned, no third-party rights) and permits replacement by a consented human recording before Gate A. Acceptance evidence for media still comes from all eight real data-room MP4s. The fixture manifest records literal file SHA-256 `4c5e8d89642cfc9658b2649362a4eda53e753fa1875a8c0c96d0aafe14fdf370`, fixture ID `stt-human-en-v1`, duration `3,399` milliseconds, language `en`, expected normalized phrase `media health check one two three`, and creator/provenance evidence hash. Normalization lowercases, strips punctuation, and maps digit groups `1-2-3` to `one two three`. RED tests require duration 2,000–5,000 ms, the exact normalized phrase, at least one non-empty segment, monotonically increasing timecodes, first start in 0–1,500 ms, and final end no later than duration +500 ms. The operator-provided CPU observation is a transcript at 0.00–2.00 seconds; Gate A independently validates the committed path and literal manifest hash before executing real faster-whisper inference, and strict mode has no asset override. Until the reviewed asset and literal hashes are committed, media preflight remains blocked.

## 11. Recorded consumer-deferral disposition

No row below may be cleared by editing an inventory label. It clears only when a RED test and a runtime signal prove producer IDs were consumed and a durable receipt exists.

| Recorded gap | Slice100 disposition | Required closing signal |
|---|---|---|
| Graph consumption by analysis/debate/scoring/Layer 2 | Implement | Each consumer output stores graph query/result IDs; graph `CR` rows and artifact provenance match |
| RAG consumption by extraction/analysis/debate/scoring/Layer 2 | Implement | `EXTRACT` owns a real document pre-index/query and `EXTRACT:rag_context` receipt; Step 19 finalizes the full index; every later consumer stores retrieval IDs |
| Conditional debate evidence retrieval | Implement | Debate node calls the real retriever; a boolean set without a call fails |
| Enrichment into debate | Implement | Debate inputs/output citations include durable enrichment fact IDs |
| Enrichment-record indexing | Implement | Index census includes every indexable durable enrichment fact; retrieval proves recovery |
| `ic_advocate` | Implement | Distinct promoted live role, durable output, request provenance, and arbiter consumption |
| Layer-2 dissent | Implement | Durable advocate/challenger disagreement and arbiter disposition; output artifact includes safe dissent |
| Deep VEP consumption | Implement | Debate/analysis/scoring/L2 record concrete VEP claim/calc/Court refs, not only package ID |
| External conflict checks | Implement | Planned checks have executed request/result/finding rows; no blocked/deferred/no-op status |
| Durable analysis/debate/scoring output | Implement | First-class PG outputs survive restart and are referenced by deliverables |

## 12. Prompt evaluation and promotion

The runtime prompt surface is derived from actual registered/executed roles and locked by a test; a hard-coded seven-ID subset cannot pass. Target surface is 19 prompt families:

- 1 generic extraction prompt.
- 1 methodology-question extraction prompt.
- 5 Layer-1 debate prompts: advocate, Sanad breaker, contradiction finder, risk officer, arbiter.
- 8 analysis prompts: financial, market, historian, team, risk officer, sector specialist, technical, terms.
- 1 scoring prompt.
- 3 Layer-2 prompts: `ic_advocate`, challenger, arbiter.

For each prompt, Slice100 creates immutable evaluation inputs/results, threshold decisions, model/config hash, evaluator identity, and approval evidence. HIGH-risk prompts require governance gates 1–4 plus OWNER and SECURITY/COMPLIANCE approvals; MEDIUM requires gates 1–2 plus OWNER approval. These must be genuine approvals—never generated placeholders. `PromptVersioningService` performs atomic promotion and durable PostgreSQL audit. The resulting `registry.prod.json` must point to the exact versions stamped by the runtime.

Offline tooling and hermetic RED tests may be implemented before Gate A. Any live model evaluation waits at Gate A. A failed evaluation or missing human approval blocks promotion and therefore blocks strict admission; the flag is never turned off to proceed.

## 13. RED-first implementation tasks

Implementation begins only after this plan is explicitly approved. Each task follows: focused RED tests → smallest production implementation → focused verification → independent review → explicit user approval before the next task. Hermetic fakes may test failure/success branches, but their results never count as acceptance evidence.

### Task 1 — external HTTP transport and canonical worker

RED tests:

- Strict acceptance rejects injected clients, `TestClient`, ASGI transport, temporary object storage, direct inline execution, and non-HTTP base URLs.
- Strict run creation returns durable `QUEUED`; only the tenant-scoped canonical worker can claim/transition it, one run per `limit=1` atomic claim.
- API and worker use the same execution service, pipeline-order version, audit sink, and tenant context.
- A resumed `COMPLETED` row is semantically revalidated.
- `IDIS_PROCESS_ROLE=api` starts no in-process worker; `worker` starts no API; absent/combined roles fail.
- Missing/expired/reused/mismatched `strict_preflight_proof_id` prevents run creation.
- Worker crash after the committed `RUNNING` claim expires and requeues the same run; a second worker claims it with a higher fencing token and resumes through rehydration.
- Heartbeat renewal succeeds only for the current owner/token before expiry. A successful renewal compare-and-set that affects zero rows fences the worker as `WORKER_LEASE_FENCED`; a database, durable-audit, connection, or transport failure that prevents the renewal transaction from completing fails closed as `WORKER_LEASE_HEARTBEAT_FAILED`. Either outcome stops the worker before output persistence or its next step, and every zombie write is rejected as `WORKER_LEASE_FENCED`.
- Injected start/completion-boundary write, audit, and commit failures roll back the native outputs, `RunStep` transition/result, heartbeat, and boundary audit together and fail as `WORKER_LEASE_BOUNDARY_COMMIT_FAILED`; each later transaction must re-establish tenant-local RLS, and no transaction may span provider execution or multiple canonical steps.
- Lease claim/reclaim preserves cooperative cancellation and the one-active-run index. At all 27 canonical cut points, RED proves that only the fully committed prefix is visible, the next step/output is absent, the second worker rehydrates from that prefix after expiry/requeue/fresh higher-token claim, the final result matches uninterrupted execution, and the stale worker cannot append a late write.

Implementation:

- Add external HTTP mode to the existing gate/harness.
- Make strict FULL run creation enqueue-only; extend `process_queued_runs.py` with supported continuous watch/poll options.
- Add the reviewed run-row worker lease, one-statement claim, database-time heartbeat/reclaim, write-path fencing, and durable lease audit proof defined in §5.1.
- Add durable bound/one-use preflight proofs and require their IDs on each strict run request.
- Task 1 containment is fail-closed while later-task native stagers are still unauthorized: the production claimed-run context uses only detached, short tenant-scoped read transactions during dispatch, rejects any direct provider-phase SQL mutation, and blocks `METHODOLOGY_DETERMINISTIC_CALCULATION`, `METHODOLOGY_EVIDENCE_TRUST_COURT`, `EXTRACT`, `GRADE`, `CALC`, `GRAPH_EVIDENCE`, `RAG_EVIDENCE`, and `DELIVERABLES` before dispatch until their reviewed completion-boundary stagers exist. This is not a go-live success condition; it prevents PostgreSQL, Neo4j, webhook, or object-store output from escaping the lease fence while Tasks 6–11 remain unapproved. The already reviewed VEP and Layer-2 native writes remain staged at the fenced completion boundary.

### Task 2 — durable dependency admission

RED each fallback independently: absent/wrong PostgreSQL schema; app superuser/bypass-RLS; JSONL/in-memory audit; SQLite/in-memory idempotency; in-memory rate limiting; temporary/relative object store; missing worker tenant; missing auth/region; non-durable provider budget.

Implementation:

- Thread `PostgresAuditSink` through every component and remove strict-path internal `InMemoryAuditSink` construction.
- Add typed readiness probes and strict startup/admission checks for PG/RLS/audit/idempotency/Redis/object store/auth/worker/budgets.
- Add migrations for missing durable entities with RLS and repository tests.
- Add the strict launcher. Prove it loads values before imports, records base-profile HMAC and audited command separately, distinguishes bootstrap/final generations, and makes final API/worker/gate HMACs match without exposing a value.
- Wire `NEO4J_DATABASE` into all sessions; add two-key auth binding, rights-approval import, and signed per-provider budget-policy import.
- Deliver an offline helper that generates the Ed25519 governance keypair and signs the rights-approval and provider-budget envelopes using the pinned RFC 8785 envelope contract. The helper must keep the private key out of reports, logs, version control, and runtime artifacts.

### Task 2b — normalize tenant RLS across the complete schema

RED tests:

- Derive the tenant-bearing inventory from a PostgreSQL catalog migrated to `0033` and require it to equal the 42-table list in §1.2; a new or missing tenant-bearing table is a plan-drift failure.
- Prove the `0033` reference defects are genuinely RED: `runs` has no `WITH CHECK`, `claims` has no explicit null guard, `tenants` has no policy, and every remaining noncanonical policy/state is reported.
- For every table, seed a valid row as the migration/bootstrap authority, then use a real `NOSUPERUSER`/`NOBYPASSRLS` application role to prove same-tenant visibility, cross-tenant and unset-context invisibility, and rejected cross-tenant/unset-context writes. Assert RLS denial rather than accepting a later unrelated constraint failure as proof.
- Prove the `tenants` self-row predicate and tenant-region lookup, and re-run worker-lease claim/heartbeat/fencing/reclaim/cancellation writes under the canonical `runs` policy.
- Prove `0033 -> 0034 -> 0033 -> 0034` on disposable PostgreSQL, including exact restoration of the pre-`0034` policy catalog and RLS/FORCE flags at the downgrade point.

Implementation:

- Add only migration `0034`: enable and force RLS on the exact 42 tables, replace all their policies with exactly one canonical `ALL` policy carrying the same null-guarded tenant predicate in both `USING` and `WITH CHECK`, and encode an exact reversible `0033` policy/state snapshot for downgrade.
- Update application tenant-metadata reads to set the transaction-local tenant context before accessing `tenants`; do not add an RLS bypass, privileged role, or alternate repository path.
- Keep every worker-lease mutation tenant-scoped and compatible with `runs` `WITH CHECK`; a fenced or cross-tenant mutation remains rejected.
- Run the focused Task 2b PostgreSQL suites, all Task 1/2 suites, the complete test tree, and the standard offline static/forbidden/diff gate in the declared environment. Stop for review with file hashes and exact pass/skip/fail counts. Do not commit Task 2b without a later explicit instruction.

### Task 3 — two-company source/package contract

RED tests fail on one deal for both folders, unexpected top-level folder count, total/count drift, cross-deal membership, emitted private folder/file names, source write, or duplicate/missing hash.

Implementation creates two isolated deals and durable source manifests, uses safe aliases/generated upload names, fingerprints the read-only source, and supports safe resumable upload ledgers.

The Task-3 source fingerprint binds safe SHA-256 locators for the exact resolved source root and each sorted top-level company folder; no raw locator is serialized. Each self-hashed durable manifest binds the complete `company/deal/documents/run/ledger/package` alias chain and every generated-name/ordinal/path-hash/content-hash/byte-count/extension/document-ID/artifact-ID row. The server independently reconciles the uploaded content hash, actual byte count, generated name, deal, document, and artifact; filesystem locator/tree digests are provenance assertions produced only by this read-only gate and are subsequently bound into the one-use strict preflight proof rather than mislabeled as server-derived facts. Preparation uses an append-only, self-hashed JSONL event ledger with contiguous sequence and previous-event hash, deterministic opaque idempotency keys, and authenticated durable deal/document/package reconciliation before any completed row is skipped. An exclusive cross-process sidecar lock encloses complete-chain validation and append; initialization and every successful save revalidate the complete persisted chain before returning. Strict package reads revalidate the persisted manifest against every durable package-file row and fail closed on drift; legacy responses retain their prior file-row shape. This uses the existing package metadata JSONB and package-file rows; it adds no table, migration, environment variable, or feature flag.

### Task 4 — all-format, large-file, encrypted/unreadable ingestion

RED tests cover PNG OCR; the exact `pdf-ocr-rescue-v1` 24-candidate matrix/order/pixels/config, primary-nonempty bypass, genuine faint-text consensus, faint-noise rejection, agreement/confidence boundaries, ambiguous/near-tie rejection, single shared deadline, max-four concurrency, cleanup, exact `ocr_rescue` provenance, and terminal-v3 rederivation; the one-document signed no-substantive-content envelope schema/signature/signer/tenant/tree/alias/content/variant/time bindings, complete-execution proof, failed/zero-span persistence, explicit 201 terminal disposition, idempotent replay, semantic-consumer exclusion, and every fail-closed branch; the TTY-only alias lookup and zero path leakage; canonical acceptance report/ledger namespace binding; both required MP4 receipts; STT-positive/frame-empty, STT-empty/frame-positive, and both-positive success; both-empty `MEDIA_NO_EXTRACTABLE_EVIDENCE`; incomplete/malformed receipt and schedule drift; `periodic-plus-scenes-v1`; the separately pinned visual frame-health manifest/hashes, exact phrase on every health sample, and real ffmpeg/Tesseract execution; all eight MP4s through local faster-whisper and frame OCR with positive-duration timecode evidence from at least one track per video; finite positive probed media duration and strict OCR/media numeric configuration; each of the three inputs over 50 MiB; bounded streaming; same-size stored-object corruption and pathname/open-file identity replacement on retry and read; bounded authenticated raw-byte streaming re-verification with no JSON/base64/full-object buffering; same-size staged-source replacement before/during parsing; original-name rename/swap/restore during media parsing; retry/resume; one aggregate parser deadline including bounded cleanup; large subprocess-result transport; zero text; the audited 81/142/0 PDF encryption dispositions; the password adapter's `none configured, none required` path; detected non-empty-password requirements with a missing/wrong password; irrecoverable corrupt/unreadable input; durable-span excerpt/hash rebinding; OCR confidence/locator proof; explicit warning-free parser proof; restart-safe reconstruction of successful PDF/XLSX/DOCX/PPTX/HTML/TEXT parser evidence; fail-closed persisted warnings/errors/span-count drift; failed confidential-spool/parser-alias cleanup; tenant-directory symlink/junction aliases; unsafe/poisoned object version identities; same-transaction ingestion completion audit rollback; process/queue/temp-file cleanup; exact bootstrap0 API/gate versus acceptance1/worker profile pairs; the exact 13-route server inventory; acceptance/canary client-specific method/path/streaming allowlists; absence of all background workers and non-ingestion routes; scoped admission that calls exactly the seven §1.6 probes and never labels worker/budgets green; launcher-only durable-mode admission and API/gate comparison of `SHA-256(base_profile_hmac)`; sustained `60/RPM` pacing independent of burst capacity; pre-upload OCR/media plus both real pinned-fixture checks; complete `src/idis/**/*.py` closure plus contract/OpenAPI/native-wrapper binding; changed per-boot UUID hash after restart; owner-authenticated persistent-byte downloads for both companies before/after restart; and the complete canary read/write/DELETE-denial matrix both before and after restart.

The §1.18 amendment additionally requires RED rederivation for `ocr-render-admission-v2` and `ocr-terminal-evidence-v4`, the real `732a18b4…` artifact through the file-backed worker and API composition root, and production-built rounding-boundary fixtures at every ladder DPI. It proves that conservative admission projection, Poppler-expected dimensions, and actual render dimensions remain distinct and that the actual raster always remains inside the unchanged resource ceiling.

The §1.11 amendment adds thematic RED modules for the exact detector pixels/order/boundaries and forbidden transforms; signed exemplar and batch schemas; deterministic all-267 prescan/no-I/O contract; candidate and reviewer-sample derivation; page-level v2 provenance; manual-v1 compatibility; multi-page exclusion versus whole-document terminal semantics; persistence/audit/idempotent replay; runtime-unseen review status; derived reconciliation-v4 counts; per-company 15-percent integer cap boundaries; readiness binding; safe-output privacy; and removal of every one-document-only assumption from source-package/gate/reconciler/OpenAPI prose. The reviewer refinement adds RED cases proving both `image_only_page` branches: a mixed document keeps its real spans and records a non-blocking hash+page locator with post-run spot-check status, while an all-image-only document remains failed with `IMAGE_ONLY_DOCUMENT_REVIEW_REQUIRED`. Mutation, duplicate locator, text contradiction, watermark overlap, and private-material cases fail closed. RED must fail because the new classifier/manifest-v2/receipt-v2/prescan/image-only/v4 behavior does not yet exist, while all existing v1 tests remain green.

The §1.22 amendment adds the production-composition real-PostgreSQL N≥3 repeated-collection RED, the production-shaped ordering-perturbation RED, the exact v6 `terminal_snapshot_digests` receipt contract, and the zero-result audit for patched-to-constant verification values across `tests/slice100_task4`. Digest equality must be proven by the production collector and canonicalizer themselves; a test that replaces a collection, canonicalizer, hash function, or frozen comparison value with a fixed digest is not evidence.

Implementation:

- Admit PNG through the authenticated upload path and route it through real OCR.
- Replace fixed 50 MiB all-bytes assumptions with a single validated `IDIS_INGEST_MAX_BYTES`, streamed hashing/storage, bounded spooling, and parser/media resource controls; prove all three data-room files over 50 MiB are admitted below the configured bound.
- Bind the read-only PDF OCR admission census to the source contract by content hash, internal exact required-page-number set, and total page count: 42 PDFs/284 zero-native-text pages are admitted. Derive classifier-first execution by subtracting each unique verified candidate page from that exact set; remove a PDF from the execution file set only when no OCR-required page bound to its hash remains, and call it classifier-terminal only when classified pages cover its complete 1..page-count domain. The present signed set proves two one-page classifier-terminal PDFs, leaving 40 PDFs/282 pages plus two PNGs that must execute OCR. Persist and reconcile every subset and both censuses; strict `IDIS_OCR_MAX_PAGES=72` covers the observed maximum without silently truncating a later page.
- For every zero-native-text PDF page, execute the single bounded `watermark-runtime-candidate-render-v1` prepass before calculating full-resolution OCR admission or calling an OCR worker. An independently passing signed-exemplar match terminates as `watermark-page-classification-v2`/`watermark_only`, emits zero spans, records no primary/rescue proof, and makes zero full-resolution OCR attempts. Only the governed below-threshold outcome proceeds through the pinned DPI ladder to primary OCR. If primary OCR then returns zero tokens, execute only the fixed `pdf-ocr-rescue-v1` matrix and accept only one corroborated same-orientation cross-transform/both-PSM clique, with exact `ocr_rescue` provenance. If that complete matrix cannot establish consensus, retain the page failure unless the exact signed manual-v1 post-execution receipt or reviewer-confirmed mixed-document `image_only_page` contract admits it; the signed-exemplar classifier is never retried after rescue, and no branch may mint text or a parsed state. Implement manifest-v2/batch/disposition verification, pre-OCR page receipt-v2, image-only page receipts, runtime-unseen review status, 15-percent cap, prescan lineage, `ocr-terminal-evidence-v3`, and terminal/durable proof v4 without a 45-degree candidate or stamp-token span. An all-image-only document remains failed and reviewer-blocked. Provide the ledger-bound TTY-only operator lookup without any report/log/stdout/stderr locator surface.
- Replace equality between the conservative admission ceiling and observed raster dimensions with the §1.18 typed Poppler 25.12.0 expected-dimension contract. Pin one resolved executable identity across readiness and worker execution, preserve projected/expected/actual dimensions and their v2/v4 digest lineage, and retain the unchanged DPI ladder and pixel bound. Exact comparison is mandatory unless reproducible pinned-version RED evidence activates the recorded one-pixel-per-axis fallback.
- Route all eight MP4s through both the configured local faster-whisper model and the plan-pinned deterministic frame-OCR policy. Persist both completed receipts per video and require at least one non-empty evidence track, with no expected STT/OCR split. Strict admission requires `IDIS_MEDIA_TIMEOUT_SECONDS=900`, `IDIS_MEDIA_MAX_DURATION_SECONDS=300`, and the exact OCR settings; the one deadline covers every media phase and cleanup. Large subprocess results cannot deadlock a join-before-drain queue, incomplete sampling cannot clear, and private frames cannot survive cleanup.
- Add a secret-safe, content-SHA-keyed password-manifest adapter. The data-room contract is `none configured, none required`: 142 encryption-marked PDFs are empty-credential-openable and zero require a non-empty password. Any unresolved password requirement, wrong configured password, or irrecoverable corruption blocks the company and never becomes a successful deferral.
- Re-hash existing object bytes before idempotent stream reuse so a same-length corruption is recovered or fails; a metadata/length match alone is never proof. Retry opens the candidate without following an untrusted alias, binds the pathname identity to the open file-descriptor/handle identity, hashes through that same open handle, and rechecks the pathname identity before reuse. A rename, swap, junction/symlink redirect, file-index/device mismatch, or identity change between pathname validation and open-file verification fails `OBJECT_INTEGRITY_FAILED`; validation may not close and reopen an unbound pathname for the reuse read.
- Re-hash persistent object bytes in bounded chunks on every object-store read, requiring exact persisted SHA-256, byte count, and stable open-file/path stat identity; corruption or replacement fails with typed `OBJECT_INTEGRITY_FAILED`. Add an authenticated durable content-integrity endpoint that invokes the compliance-enforced bounded verifier and returns only `durable-object-integrity-v1` safe proof: exact content SHA-256, size, object version, backend, and SHA-256 of the persisted URI. Raw URI/path and bytes are never returned. The terminal collector binds this receipt to each source/document/artifact row, removes the raw URI, and the reconciler requires 267 unique object versions and URI hashes on one persistent backend. This adds no table, migration, environment variable, or feature flag; a missing, malformed, non-persistent, or mismatched proof fails `DOCUMENT_CONTENT_INTEGRITY_FAILED`/`DATA_ROOM_RECONCILIATION_INCOMPLETE`.
- Re-read the staged source with bounded hashing before parsing and after parser/external-process access, requiring exact route size/SHA-256 and stable file identity/stat fields. For media, create a parser-owned same-filesystem hard-link to the verified inode, verify the source/alias identity and digest before and after inference, and parse only that alias; swapping and restoring the caller-owned staging name therefore cannot redirect ffprobe/STT. Any replacement, mutation, or unremovable parser alias fails `corrupted_file`; parser spans can never be persisted against a different stored-object digest.
- Treat private-spool removal as a success boundary: the route proves cleanup before downstream idempotency can snapshot HTTP 201, the staging middleware retries cleanup for error/replay paths, and an unproven cleanup returns only safe `INGEST_STAGING_CLEANUP_FAILED`/HTTP 500 with no path or filesystem detail.
- Reject any filesystem object-store tenant/object path whose resolved identity differs from its exact lexical tenant namespace, including an in-base symlink or Windows junction from tenant A to tenant B; recheck after directory creation before publication. The same guard covers put, streamed put, get, head, version listing, and delete. Explicit and `_latest` version IDs must be canonical UUID spellings before any path interpolation, and loaded metadata must exactly match the requested tenant/key/version before read, reuse, listing, or deletion.
- Propagate `AuditSinkError` from both streamed and byte ingestion so the real HTTP request becomes a 5xx and its PostgreSQL transaction rolls back; discard in-process artifact/document/span mirrors on any persistence/audit exception. Every ingestion-completion audit write uses the same RLS-scoped PostgreSQL request transaction and connection as the corresponding artifact/document/span terminal writes; a separately committed audit-sink transaction cannot certify completion. A completion-row or completion-audit failure rolls the shared transaction back, so a failed durable audit can never be converted into a 4xx while completed-looking ingestion rows commit.
- Provide the authenticated durable-row collector and fail-closed reconciler for exactly 267 terminal source objects across the two isolated company ledgers, including source-bound OCR hashes, parser/encryption dispositions, runtime provenance, durable spans, every PNG, every MP4, every large-file row, every signed manual terminal exclusion, and every classifier page receipt. Every ordinary page is real and span-bearing; only an exact manual-v1 or classifier-v2 `DOCUMENT_NO_SUBSTANTIVE_CONTENT_CONFIRMED` proof may exclude a page, and only a document with no remaining substantive page may remain failed/zero-span. Manual, batch-attested, and runtime-unseen counts and the v4 receipt digest are separately derived; runtime-unseen keeps final acceptance review-required. The safe durable document summary exposes `parsed_doc_type` separately from the public artifact classification, and the collector requires the exact source-format binding (`PDF/XLSX/DOCX/PPTX/HTML/TEXT/IMAGE/VIDEO`) before admitting a row. Legacy span rows may expose a required-but-null `content_hash`; acceptance rows must still carry a valid 64-hex hash. Before reducing private text from the safe receipt, the collector requires a non-empty excerpt and recomputes its SHA-256 against that hash; it also requires an explicit empty `parse_warning_codes` list. Task 4 wires and executes this ingestion-only seam through the real external strict API, persists only its safe aggregate/digest receipt, proves restart survival and tenant isolation, and freezes the result for Task 13 to consume. It does not execute a FULL run, worker, LLM, enrichment provider, or later-task acceptance surface.
- Make every SQL read feeding terminal collection explicitly and totally ordered with a unique final tie-breaker, then normalize every scalar and unordered collection through `task4-terminal-snapshot-v1`. Produce the §1.22 top digest and exact five safe component digests, persist both compared mappings before any mismatch failure, and freeze the complete mapping across restart. No database-plan, mapping-insertion, locale, or process-hash ordering is evidence.
- Require the strict launcher to pass the reviewed gate profile directly into durable mode. The authenticated API readiness response and gate receipt expose only `SHA-256(base_profile_hmac)`; the gate compares its launcher-derived hash with the API hash before either proof phase and blocks a mismatch. Before the first upload, require healthy OCR/media configuration, real inference over the immutable pinned STT fixture, and real ffmpeg/Tesseract execution over the separately pinned visual frame fixture. Pace the external client at the admitted sustained integration rate (`60 / RPM` seconds); burst multiplies capacity only. Bind the sorted recursive full `src/idis/**/*.py` closure plus `contracts/contract_lock.json`, `openapi/IDIS_OpenAPI_v6_3.yaml`, `scripts/invoke_strict_native_process.ps1`, and `scripts/strict_process_identity.psm1` into the code digest and fail on any member/path-set drift.
- Derive `task4-ingestion-v1` only from the reviewed bootstrap0 API/gate pair. At API construction, mount and census exactly the 13 method/templates in §1.6 and remove docs/OpenAPI/metrics and every non-ingestion route; any missing, duplicate, or extra route fails startup. The external client separately enforces canonical UUID paths, exact owner/canary method sets, and streaming only for `GET .../content` before transport. Run exactly the seven scoped dependency probes and no worker/budget probe, and start/stop no pipeline, webhook, or janitor background process. The normal acceptance1 API surface and complete admission remain unchanged.
- Mint a fresh API-boot UUID in memory on every API process start and expose it only through its SHA-256 in the safe authenticated readiness/proof surface. The restart phase requires a different boot hash and zero upload POSTs. In both phases, the owner must incrementally hash a bounded raw-byte streaming durable PostgreSQL/object-store-backed download for each safe company alias; JSON/base64/full-object buffering is forbidden. The canary must receive the canonical auth/RLS denial on every planned cross-tenant read and applicable write probe, including document `DELETE`, both before and after restart, never an unrelated validation failure. Safe byte totals and media-track-positive counts are recomputed from durable rows; no observed media split or frame-health `5/5` observation is encoded as an acceptance constant.
- Reconstruct restart-safe native parser success only from a durable `PARSED` row, completed parser runtime, explicit empty warning/error lists, an exact positive total persisted span count, and an exact parser-to-canonical-row type binding: native identities map to themselves, `IMAGE -> IMAGE`, and `MEDIA -> VIDEO`. Emit the format-specific completion reason and READY status only for that complete evidence. OCR evidence flags and the media dual-track success proof require the same durable status/code-list/span/type binding and can never override a failed row or failed runtime; either individual media receipt may be completed with zero spans, but the two-track union must have positive durable evidence. Any persisted warning, known or unknown error, missing parser evidence, mismatched span proof, or parser/canonical-type mismatch remains corrupted/ineligible and fail-closed instead of falling back to a permissive legacy capability. Direct non-persisted capability inspection retains its filename-based behavior.
- Persist native HTML and TEXT parses under their exact `DocumentType` identities. Migration `0035` widens only `valid_document_doc_type`; downgrade blocks rather than relabeling while either identity exists and otherwise restores the exact prior constraint. No table, column, index, trigger, environment variable, feature flag, or Task-7 schema is added.

### Task 5 — durable CDD+FDD import/freeze/select

RED tests fail on CDD-only selection, 78/80 FDD rows, wrong header row, mutable version, source-hash drift, duplicate row, missing sheet/row trace, in-memory repository, absent run selection, or cross-tenant read.

RED tests fail on `extractor=None`, synthetic executor, `external_calls_enabled=False`, no provider request proof, missing applicable task, empty output, internal partial/failed result, rejected source locator, non-canonical claim, or missing Sanad.

Implementation adds the task-aware promoted Anthropic extractor, durable attempts/results, canonical row-linked claims/evidence/Sanads, and merges IDs into the main evidence path. No fallback is present.

### Task 7 — central 28-step semantic contract and order version

RED matrix tests exercise every step's typed failure invariant. Global RED cases include nested `FAILED`/`PARTIAL`/`DEFERRED`/`BLOCKED`, skipped/not-attempted, `all_failed`, nonzero rejected/failed/deferred/blocked counts, `durable_export=false`, missing producer persistence, and missing consumer receipt.

Implementation adds:

- Before any Task 7 migration is written, return the complete receipt/edge schema design (keys, constraints, RLS, lifecycle, backfill, downgrade, and erasure classification) for explicit review. Its backfill migration must pass upgrade -> downgrade -> upgrade on disposable PostgreSQL.

- A staged `StepSemanticValidator`: dispatch-only typed validation; one atomic output-root/expectation/incoming-receipt/completion transaction; resume validation of completed output, incoming receipts, and currently derivable outgoing expectations only; and independent three-set contract derivation/equality at finalization.
- Stable safe error codes stored in `RunStep.error_code`.
- `snapshot-v1`, `full-v1`, immutable historical `full-strict-v16` and `full-strict-v17`, and the append-frozen target `full-strict-v18` registry entries. V16 and v17 remain `INACTIVE`, explicitly superseded, and never selectable; v18 remains `INACTIVE` until the complete activation census and a separate reviewed activation change pass. Run/step version+hash columns, immutable selection, version-aware dispatch/order/resume/finalization, durable per-step loaders, exact 8/28/28 cardinality, and explicit exclusion of auxiliary `RUN_LIFECYCLE` from canonical counts remain mandatory. Earlier full-strict identifiers and hashes are never compatibility aliases or admission fallbacks.
- A first-class successful `run_step_outputs` root for every canonical step/subrole, including status-only checks and provider `MISS`; semantic completion is atomic with output-root creation, and receipts can only target those concrete outputs.
- Stop/restart RED tests at every one of the 27 step cut points use lease expiry, same-row requeue, and a fresh higher-token claim; rehydrated typed state and final output must match uninterrupted execution, and the stale worker's write must be fenced.
- No generic status flip or inventory-row edit.

RED tests cover all named formulas, Decimal/currency/period validation, input row/claim lineage, missing inputs, zero/invalid denominator, version/hash stability, replay reproducibility, CalcSanad, 79-row financial output coverage, and No-Free-Facts.

Implementation adds row-specific formula selection and makes `financial_diligence.json` expose every FDD row's answer/gap, evidence/Sanad, red flag, formula inputs/output, and lineage.

RED tests reject deal UUID queries, operator-seed-as-identity, rights selection before verified identity/applicability, missing required identifiers, `UNKNOWN` or missing-ticker false N/A, wrong identity version, listed-plus-revoked rights, credential/budget/cache/HTTP/fact side effects on an N/A branch, DEV rights mode, unencrypted credentials, unapproved applicable RED/YELLOW rights, config-only health, discarded facts, in-memory audit/cache as persistence, absent RAG index, or absent consumer receipt. They derive the expected provider/rights/budget/probe inventory from the authoritative registry; require EPO OAuth, Semantic Scholar rights/attribution, and Perplexity commercial entitlement when applicable; require two identity-bound zero-call N/A receipts instead of Finnhub/FMP probes when both frozen acceptance identities are positively private/unlisted; reject Perplexity prose as primary evidence; require citation-backed low-tier evidence/Sanads; verify the three approved specialist-routing maps plus scoring, conflict, every Layer-2 role, and applicable deliverable subroles; and reject any external-provider edge or receipt into Layer 1.

Implementation adds durable verified identity and immutable applicability decisions first, then the exact §1.7 authorization/execution order, provider-specific query construction, the registry-derived 18 provider readiness rows (`provider-probes-v1` for applicable providers; identity/rule-bound zero-call N/A receipts for inapplicable providers), `provider_probe_receipts`, signed commercial/attribution approval import and RLS rows, real preflight execution, durable normalized facts/provenance, RAG indexing, and downstream wiring for every table row in section 9. Registry metadata—not literals—drives probe/N/A, rights, budget, expected-edge, and Gate-A inventories.

RED tests prove that a boolean/status label or package ID alone cannot clear a gap. They fail when internal-evidence graph/RAG inputs are absent from Layer-1 debate/analysis/scoring/L2, when external enrichment is absent from its applicable analysis/scoring/L2/conflict/deliverable consumers, when enrichment facts are not indexed, when external context leaks into Court/VEP/Layer-1 debate, when conflict checks are only planned, or when VEP content is not dereferenced.

Court/VEP execute over internal evidence only; external enrichment joins after the VEP boundary through the approved analysis, scoring, conflict-check, Layer-2, and deliverable routes.

RED tests require eight analysis agents, five Layer-1 roles, Layer-2 advocate/challenger/arbiter, promoted prompt stamps, real request provenance, namespace-correct graph/RAG/VEP inputs, routed enrichment for applicable analysis and Layer-2 roles, zero enrichment input to every Layer-1 role, explicit dissent, durable outputs/Muḥāsabah, and successful gates.

Only then may the complete 48-rule three-way equality

RED tests derive all runtime prompt IDs and fail on an unregistered role, missing gate evidence, failed thresholds, absent required human approvals, wrong hash/version/risk class, non-atomic pointer, or runtime/pointer mismatch.

Implementation adds the methodology and Layer-2 advocate prompt artifacts, evaluation tooling integrated into the existing gate, durable promotion audit, and final `IDIS_REQUIRE_PROMOTED_PROMPTS=1` admission.

RED tests require the target CLI flags, external transport, fixed root/count contract, two-company isolation, safe resume, stop-on-first-failure, no secret/private output, and nonzero exit on any blocker.

Implementation extends `scripts/run_real_example_gate.py` with target-state options used in section 16, including strict-profile launcher operation, preparation/preflight-proof ledgers, separate owner/canary key env names, and one-use proof binding. It derives the exact expected PDF-OCR content-hash set from the read-only source contract, invokes the Task-4 authenticated durable collector/reconciler, and binds their safe contract digests into the preparation proof. There is no strict fallback client. Safe reports are append-only and contain only allowed aggregate fields.

### Task 14 — per-company package/restart/isolation verifier

RED tests fail on any missing/extra catalog row, wrong content type/size/hash, missing URI/object, unauthenticated access, download mismatch, post-restart failure, tenant existence oracle, cross-deal ID, unreferenced fact, or leakage token.

Implementation verifies all 15 rows, 14 manifest entries, authenticated streaming hashes, durable URIs, process restart, authenticated canary-tenant denial using the distinct key, source immutability, and safe-output leakage.

### Task 15 — offline capstone and independent reviews

**15A, before Gate A:** run the offline repository gate, migration upgrade/downgrade/upgrade against disposable PostgreSQL, real disposable PostgreSQL/RLS/Redis/filesystem tests, and independent code/runtime-path/security/leakage/plan-conformance reviews. The explicitly approved Task-4 exception also runs the dedicated local ingestion-only PostgreSQL/API/persistent-object profile and all 267 read-only uploads through parser, durable-object, span, restart, and tenant-isolation verification. It starts no worker, performs no FULL run, and calls no LLM, embedding, graph, enrichment, or other external provider. No other configured acceptance service is contacted before Gate A. A skipped required offline or Task-4 ingestion-proof test is not green.

**15B, after Gate A and before private preparation/Gate B:** run the configured PostgreSQL/RLS/Redis/object-store/Neo4j/pgvector/API/worker/provider/model/media integration checks and final-profile conformance checks. A skipped configured-service test is not green. This phase cannot be claimed complete before Gate A.

Only Tasks 1–14 and Task 15A must pass before pausing at Gate A. Task 15B must pass after Gate A before the preparation command may mint `may_proceed=true` proofs.

## 14. Approval gates and stop rules

### Gate A — live preflight/evaluation approval

Immediately before the first connection to configured semantic/provider acceptance services, stop and ask the user. The only pre-Gate-A carve-out is the separately approved Task-4 dedicated local ingestion profile described in sections 1.6, 13, and 15A; its migration, scoped API-only admission/process, local OCR/STT/frame OCR, persistent object writes, and 267-file proof do not import or claim provider governance, authorize or execute a worker/FULL run, or perform a provider call. Before approval, do not run the final acceptance-profile migration/import/start sequence or perform a real network/model/provider probe. The safe request shows the migration revision, service/provider/model names, maximum call counts, timeout/budget caps, expected small cost class, and the frozen Task-4 proof for 267 files split 58/209 with OCR/STT/resource bounds. This makes Gate A unambiguously precede `check_llm_model_health(run_probe=True)` and every other semantic/provider live preflight.

Gate A authorizes only:

- Applying the reviewed Slice100 migrations to the configured acceptance PostgreSQL, importing the signed rights/budget policies, and starting the role-separated API/worker.
- `check_llm_model_health(run_probe=True)` for every configured model role.
- Bounded real OpenAI embedding, Neo4j, PG/RLS, Redis, object-store, OCR/STT, and all registry-derived 18 provider health probes. A failed provider probe is a hard Gate-A blocker.
- Bounded non-private live prompt evaluations.
- After successful probes/promotion and final-profile restart, revalidate or safely reuse the unchanged Task-4 two-deal durable corpus only if its source, ledger, code, plan, profile, parser receipts, object/span identities, restart digest, and isolation proof still match; otherwise rerun the authenticated read-only-source ingestion proof. Then execute private preflight and mint the two bound `may_proceed=true` proofs for all 267 files. This does not authorize a FULL run or private LLM/provider analysis.

It does not authorize the two private FULL runs. Any failed probe/evaluation stops. Prompt promotion proceeds only after the required real human approvals are present. The API/worker are then restarted with `IDIS_REQUIRE_PROMOTED_PROMPTS=1`; the frozen Task-4 corpus is revalidated/reused only under the exact binding above (or reingested if any binding changed), and the final strict preflight must report `may_proceed=true` for each company.

### Gate B — full priced-run approval

Immediately before queuing `company-001`, stop and ask the user again. The safe request shows per-company file/task counts, exact provider/model maximum calls, durable budget balance/cap, expected artifact count, and both fresh `may_proceed=true` preflight-proof IDs. Each proof is bound to its tenant/deal/corpus/profile/pipeline/methodology/prompts/probe receipts and expiry.

After approval:

1. Submit Company 1's one-use proof ID with the queue request, atomically validate/consume it, and complete `company-001` (58 files). Stop on its first failure.
2. Only if every acceptance check for Company 1 passes, queue and complete `company-002` (209 files). Stop on its first failure.
3. Perform package verification, restart both API and worker, then repeat authenticated status/ledger/manifest/download/hash/isolation verification.

There is no automatic retry of a priced semantic/provider failure. A retry requires diagnosis, a bounded fix, offline verification, and renewed approval if it creates new provider spend.

## 15. Global fail-closed conditions

The gate exits nonzero and no run is claimed successful if any of the following occurs:

- The source root/counts/formats change, source write is detected, folders are combined, or file membership crosses deals.
- Any file is missing, unsupported, oversize, encrypted without a valid secret, unreadable, corrupt, deferred, OCR/STT/frame-OCR pending, has zero total durable evidence spans without the exact signed `DOCUMENT_NO_SUBSTANTIVE_CONTENT_CONFIRMED` terminal receipt, lacks either completed media receipt, is missing its authenticated bounded persistent-object integrity receipt, or is not durably reconciled. A confirmed-no-substantive-content row is an explicit non-consumable exclusion and cannot satisfy any evidence/agent/debate/scoring requirement; its exact variant must be durably bound and separately counted. `image_only_page` never satisfies the zero-span exception: it is admissible only when its parent document has another independently verified substantive span, and an all-image-only document fails `IMAGE_ONLY_DOCUMENT_REVIEW_REQUIRED`.
- The watermark policy/exemplar/runtime-candidate/manifest/batch/prescan/review-disposition/sample binding drifts; a pre-OCR classifier page bypasses zero-native-text admission or the one bounded classifier render, performs any full-resolution OCR/primary/rescue work, fabricates an OCR terminal proof, or lacks its v2 pre-OCR receipt; an `image_only_page` bypasses zero native text, a below-threshold classifier result, admitted primary-empty execution, or complete rescue no-consensus; a v3 OCR/preclassified page set overlaps, omits an admitted required page, or drifts from its page-bound render-admission list/digest; any of `pdf_ocr_admission_file_count`, `pdf_ocr_admission_page_count`, `pdf_classifier_first_file_count`, `pdf_classifier_first_page_count`, `pdf_classifier_only_file_count`, `pdf_classifier_only_page_count`, `pdf_classifier_terminal_file_count`, `pdf_ocr_file_count`, `pdf_ocr_execution_page_count`, or `ocr_file_count` fails the exact §1.3 derivation; a score or faintness bound is rounded into success; an excluded page emits a span; one page is claimed by both terminal classes; the per-company 15-percent cap is exceeded; a prescan page lacks a reviewer disposition; document 00054 is missing from the candidate set; a batch member drifts; an image-only record lacks its safe hash/locator; an all-image-only document is marked parsed; or a runtime-unseen candidate is omitted from the safe review-required report. Runtime-unseen ingestion may finish, but the Task-4 acceptance claim remains blocked until reviewer confirmation.
- An OCR page conflates its conservative admission ceiling with Poppler-expected dimensions; lacks exact projected/expected/actual dimensions or v2/v4 digest lineage; uses a Poppler version/executable other than the admitted pinned identity; exceeds the actual pixel bound; or accepts any expected/actual delta outside the exact or explicitly evidenced one-pixel fallback contract.
- A fresh PDF lacks `ocr-terminal-evidence-v5` and its `pdf-page-disposition-census-v1`; the census omits or duplicates a page, assigns one page to more than one of `native_text`/`ocr_text`/`image_only_page`/`watermark_only`, contains a class/evidence-binding contradiction, or fails canonical digest rederivation; an OCR-performed zero-text page lacks a complete image-only render/no-consensus receipt; or a PDF is marked `PARSED` without at least one native- or OCR-text page.
- A terminal-collection SQL read lacks an explicit total order with a unique tie-breaker; canonical scalar or collection rendering is ambiguous; any of the `documents`, `artifacts`, `spans`, `censuses`, or `audit_counts` component digests is missing/malformed; the top digest does not bind their exact mapping; repeated unchanged collections differ; a mismatch is raised before both safe component mappings and the differing section names are persisted; restart omits the full mapping from frozen comparison; or a Task-4 test masks a verification value with a constant digest.
- API transport is not external HTTP; run did not traverse middleware/queue/worker; or API/worker used different strict profiles.
- The strict launcher did not load the profile before imports, process roles are ambiguous, the API starts an in-process worker, bootstrap/final generations are confused, the bootstrap API exposes anything outside the exact Task-4 scope, or final API/worker/gate base-profile HMACs differ.
- A run lacks a fresh one-use preflight proof bound to the exact tenant/deal/corpus/profile/pipeline/methodology/prompts/probe receipt set.
- PostgreSQL/RLS/Alembic, audit, idempotency, Redis, object store, auth/residency, worker scope, or provider budget is missing, unhealthy, fallback, non-durable, or unproven when required by the active scope. Task-4 scoped admission may omit only worker/provider-budget checks and must record them as outside scope rather than passed.
- Worker lease configuration is missing/invalid, claim is not one atomic state transition, heartbeat cannot renew, expiry/reclaim is unproven, or stale-token writes are not durably fenced.
- Either methodology/version is missing; a row/task/result/claim/evidence/Sanad/formula/coverage link is absent; or a methodology output is empty.
- Any step has an internal non-success status/count, missing persistence proof, or missing required non-empty output; any consumer completes without its full incoming-receipt set; any producer/output/decision lacks its complete derived expectation set; or finalization finds an expected edge without exactly one correctly bound receipt.
- An applicable provider lacks credential/rights/identity, evaluates rights before verified identity/applicability, fails its preflight/runtime call, lacks provenance/persistence/consumer proof, or is labeled deferred. Verified `NOT_APPLICABLE` never substitutes for a missing identifier and is valid only under §1.7 with immutable positive identity proof and zero rights/credential/budget/cache/transport/fact side effects.
- A graph/RAG/enrichment/VEP/debate/agent/score/L2 consumer edge lacks concrete producer IDs.
- Any runtime prompt is unregistered, unevaluated, unapproved, non-PROD, mismatched, or unstamped; `IDIS_REQUIRE_PROMOTED_PROMPTS` is not literal `1`.
- A required agent/role did not make a real call, used deterministic output, failed Muḥāsabah, or lacks durable output/dissent.
- Deliverables are not exactly the catalog, `durable_export` is false, an object/URI/hash/content type/download is wrong, restart loses state, or isolation/leakage fails.

## 16. Exact target-state command sequence

Section 16.0 is the separately approved Task-4 ingestion-only sequence under the current authorization. Sections 16.1 onward describe the later Task-13/full-live target state after Tasks 1–15. Flags marked here are implementation targets and do not exist at the pinned base. Secret values stay in the approved dotenv/secret store and are never echoed or passed as arguments.

### 16.0 Current Task-4 acceptance-015 ingestion and restart verification

The offline prescan, reviewer dispositions, and signed batch prerequisite were completed before `acceptance-004` and remain hash-bound unchanged. They are historical prerequisites, not runnable steps in the current sequence: `acceptance-015` neither regenerates nor relabels prescan evidence or review renders. The executable sequence begins with the exact sealed `acceptance-014` evidence in §1.27, strict-profile, source-fingerprint, and fresh-namespace preflight below. The pre-Gate-A ingestion exception uses the reviewed `bootstrap-v1` strict profile with `IDIS_REQUIRE_PROMOTED_PROMPTS=0`, the dedicated PostgreSQL database, persistent object store, and only the local services authorized in sections 1.6 and 15A. For command `api` or this exact durable gate mode, that HMAC-bound pair intrinsically selects `task4-ingestion-v1`; no flag or ambient toggle can broaden it. No command below imports provider governance, starts a worker/background dispatcher/janitor, queues a run, mints a full-live preflight proof, or calls an LLM, embedding, graph, enrichment, prompt, debate, methodology, scoring, or deliverable provider. Execution remains fail-closed before namespace rotation until the approved §1.22 snapshot-stability, §1.24 shared receipt-whitelist, §1.26 native-process wrapper, and §1.27 Windows dual-PID contracts plus the complete declared-environment verification are green.

Before any namespace write or protected-profile rotation, reverify the sealed `acceptance-014` member set/hashes, both dead recorded processes and closed port, immutable source aggregates, protected-profile ACL/read-only state, and absence/emptiness of every `acceptance-015` namespace exactly as required by §1.27. Only after that read-only preflight is green, complete the owner-only/read-only strict-profile update described there. The edit changes only `IDIS_DATABASE_URL`, `IDIS_DATABASE_ADMIN_URL`, `IDIS_OBJECT_STORE_BASE_DIR`, and `IDIS_REDIS_URL` from the sealed acceptance-014 namespaces to `idis_acceptance_015`, `acceptance-015`, and Redis DB 14. It proves the key set and every other value byte-identical, writes once, restores read-only, and re-proves both ACL checks. The bootstrap generation and promoted-prompts setting remain unchanged. The following wrapper-captured preflight is the required post-rotation recheck before report-directory creation; it repeats the source, ACL, profile-target, and namespace-freshness proofs. Its two capture files are staged beside the absent report root, then moved into the new root only after every check passes.

```powershell
Set-StrictMode -Version Latest
$ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'
$Worktree = 'C:\Projects\IDIS\IDIS-slice100'
$PriorRoot = 'C:\Projects\IDIS\IDIS\.local_reports\slice100\acceptance-014'
$Task4ReportRoot = 'C:\Projects\IDIS\IDIS\.local_reports\slice100\acceptance-015'
$StrictNativeProcess = Join-Path $Worktree 'scripts\invoke_strict_native_process.ps1'
$Python = 'C:\Projects\IDIS\IDIS\.venv\Scripts\python.exe'
$PreflightStdout = "$Task4ReportRoot.preflight.stdout.log"
$PreflightStderr = "$Task4ReportRoot.preflight.stderr.log"
$ExpectedPriorHashes = @{
  '.api.pid.lock' = '6e340b9cffb37a989ca544e6bb780a2c78901d3fb33738768511a30617afa01d'
  'api.initial.stderr.log' = 'ae25b68ebc7e476788a0a8edcc96ba3b2a15601036145e4127b0519dcaf9cc61'
  'api.initial.stdout.log' = 'e3b0c44298fc1c149afbf4c8996fb92427ae41e4649b934ca495991b7852b855'
  'api.pid' = '4e3b40474e2048eff584dcbb2d4a729f1e6e07f48ff664986e6c9fa9aa9f85c4'
  'migration.stderr.log' = 'e3b0c44298fc1c149afbf4c8996fb92427ae41e4649b934ca495991b7852b855'
  'migration.stdout.log' = 'e3b0c44298fc1c149afbf4c8996fb92427ae41e4649b934ca495991b7852b855'
  'preflight.stderr.log' = 'e3b0c44298fc1c149afbf4c8996fb92427ae41e4649b934ca495991b7852b855'
  'preflight.stdout.log' = '83bf38d0ff0f5d24907e8d09c5c82bc8ac20c620fe9fe8a16394a416c7816126'
  'reconciliation.stderr.log' = 'e3b0c44298fc1c149afbf4c8996fb92427ae41e4649b934ca495991b7852b855'
  'reconciliation.stdout.log' = '7d6d7a56408e151caa2a7a86122485e844dfe058e64dfd875355d2626513e752'
  'reconciliation_log.jsonl' = 'fd7bfe8f2a286c3d89fe80d44d1491d6740c7ddbf6c82d486d5c349a9db2d896'
}
$ExpectedPriorMembers = @($ExpectedPriorHashes.Keys | Sort-Object)
$ObservedPriorMembers = @(Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $PriorRoot -Force | ForEach-Object Name | Sort-Object)
if (Compare-Object -ReferenceObject $ExpectedPriorMembers -DifferenceObject $ObservedPriorMembers) { throw 'ACCEPTANCE_014_EVIDENCE_MEMBER_SET_DRIFT' }
if (-not ((Get-Item -LiteralPath $PriorRoot -Force).Attributes -band [IO.FileAttributes]::ReadOnly)) { throw 'ACCEPTANCE_014_EVIDENCE_NOT_READ_ONLY' }
foreach ($Name in $ExpectedPriorHashes.Keys) {
  $Path = Join-Path $PriorRoot $Name
  if ((Get-FileHash -LiteralPath $Path -Algorithm SHA256).Hash.ToLowerInvariant() -ne $ExpectedPriorHashes[$Name]) { throw 'ACCEPTANCE_014_EVIDENCE_DRIFT' }
  if (-not ((Get-Item -LiteralPath $Path -Force).Attributes -band [IO.FileAttributes]::ReadOnly)) { throw 'ACCEPTANCE_014_EVIDENCE_NOT_READ_ONLY' }
}
if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $Task4ReportRoot) { throw 'TASK4_REPORT_NAMESPACE_NOT_FRESH' }
if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $PreflightStdout) { throw 'TASK4_PREFLIGHT_STDOUT_NOT_FRESH' }
if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $PreflightStderr) { throw 'TASK4_PREFLIGHT_STDERR_NOT_FRESH' }
if ([Net.NetworkInformation.IPGlobalProperties]::GetIPGlobalProperties().GetActiveTcpListeners() | Where-Object Port -EQ 8000) { throw 'TASK4_API_PORT_ALREADY_LISTENING' }

Set-Location -LiteralPath $Worktree
$env:PYTHONPATH = "$Worktree\src"
$env:IDIS_STRICT_DOTENV_PATH = '<reviewed-task4-ingestion-secret-dotenv-path>'
$Task4PreflightCode = @'
import os, stat, sys
from pathlib import Path
import redis, sqlalchemy
from idis.evaluation.real_example_source_package import build_two_company_source_contract
from idis.runtime.profile import load_strict_runtime_profile
from idis.runtime.secure_files import path_acl_is_current_owner_only
def require(value, code):
    if not value:
        raise SystemExit(code)
require(sys.flags.optimize == 0, 'PYTHON_OPTIMIZATION_FORBIDDEN')
s = build_two_company_source_contract(root=Path(r'C:\Projects\IDIS\IDIS\real_example'))
require(s.source_tree_fingerprint == 'dc29e07e7c605a7ba4bd3b84ad9ab481c32056e544f054a64f95946a460ef64d', 'TASK4_SOURCE_FINGERPRINT_DRIFT')
require(s.total_file_count == 267 and s.total_bytes == 858207454, 'TASK4_SOURCE_AGGREGATE_DRIFT')
require([(x.file_count, x.total_bytes) for x in s.companies] == [(58, 496782706), (209, 361424748)], 'TASK4_SOURCE_COMPANY_AGGREGATE_DRIFT')
p = load_strict_runtime_profile(command='migrate')
f = Path(os.environ['IDIS_STRICT_DOTENV_PATH']).resolve()
require(path_acl_is_current_owner_only(f) and path_acl_is_current_owner_only(f.parent, directory=True), 'STRICT_PROFILE_ACL_INVALID')
require(bool(getattr(f.stat(), 'st_file_attributes', 0) & stat.FILE_ATTRIBUTE_READONLY), 'STRICT_PROFILE_NOT_READ_ONLY')
a = sqlalchemy.engine.make_url(p.values['IDIS_DATABASE_ADMIN_URL'])
u = sqlalchemy.engine.make_url(p.values['IDIS_DATABASE_URL'])
o = Path(p.values['IDIS_OBJECT_STORE_BASE_DIR'])
q = redis.ConnectionPool.from_url(p.values['IDIS_REDIS_URL'], protocol=2)
require(a.database == u.database == 'idis_acceptance_015', 'TASK4_DATABASE_NAMESPACE_INVALID')
require(o.name == 'acceptance-015' and not o.exists(), 'TASK4_OBJECT_NAMESPACE_NOT_FRESH')
require(q.connection_kwargs.get('db') == 14, 'TASK4_REDIS_NAMESPACE_INVALID')
e = sqlalchemy.create_engine(a.set(database='postgres'))
with e.connect() as c:
    x = c.execute(sqlalchemy.text("SELECT 1 FROM pg_database WHERE datname='idis_acceptance_015'")).scalar_one_or_none()
e.dispose()
require(x is None, 'TASK4_DATABASE_NAMESPACE_NOT_FRESH')
r = redis.Redis(connection_pool=q)
require(r.dbsize() == 0, 'TASK4_REDIS_NAMESPACE_NOT_FRESH')
r.close(); q.disconnect()
print('TASK4_ACCEPTANCE_015_NAMESPACE_PREFLIGHT_GREEN')
'@
$Preflight = & $StrictNativeProcess `
  -FilePath $Python `
  -ArgumentList @(
    '-c',
    $Task4PreflightCode
  ) `
  -WorkingDirectory $Worktree `
  -StandardOutputPath $PreflightStdout `
  -StandardErrorPath $PreflightStderr
if ($Preflight.ExitCode -ne 0) { throw 'TASK4_NAMESPACE_PREFLIGHT_FAILED' }
```

The Python check hashes the source tree read-only and performs only read-only profile/PostgreSQL/Redis/object freshness probes. The wrapper captures every byte of both streams without interpreting stderr. A failure stops before report-root/database/object creation. After success, create the report root, preserve both preflight streams inside it, and migrate exactly through `0035`:

```powershell
Set-StrictMode -Version Latest
$ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'
$Worktree = 'C:\Projects\IDIS\IDIS-slice100'
$Task4ReportRoot = 'C:\Projects\IDIS\IDIS\.local_reports\slice100\acceptance-015'
$StrictNativeProcess = Join-Path $Worktree 'scripts\invoke_strict_native_process.ps1'
$Python = 'C:\Projects\IDIS\IDIS\.venv\Scripts\python.exe'
Set-Location -LiteralPath $Worktree
$env:PYTHONPATH = "$Worktree\src"
$env:IDIS_STRICT_DOTENV_PATH = '<reviewed-task4-ingestion-secret-dotenv-path>'
if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $Task4ReportRoot) { throw 'TASK4_REPORT_NAMESPACE_NOT_FRESH' }
New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $Task4ReportRoot | Out-Null
Move-Item -LiteralPath "$Task4ReportRoot.preflight.stdout.log" -Destination "$Task4ReportRoot\preflight.stdout.log"
Move-Item -LiteralPath "$Task4ReportRoot.preflight.stderr.log" -Destination "$Task4ReportRoot\preflight.stderr.log"

$Migration = & $StrictNativeProcess `
  -FilePath $Python `
  -ArgumentList @(
    '-m',
    'idis.runtime.strict_launcher',
    'migrate',
    '--alembic-config',
    'src/idis/persistence/migrations/alembic.ini',
    '--upgrade',
    '0035'
  ) `
  -WorkingDirectory $Worktree `
  -StandardOutputPath "$Task4ReportRoot\migration.stdout.log" `
  -StandardErrorPath "$Task4ReportRoot\migration.stderr.log"
if ($Migration.ExitCode -ne 0) { throw 'TASK4_MIGRATION_FAILED' }
```

Start only the real external Task-4 API. Background wrapper success means spawn only; PID-file, process, port, and strict launcher admission remain required before the gate runs:

```powershell
Set-StrictMode -Version Latest
$ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'
$Worktree = 'C:\Projects\IDIS\IDIS-slice100'
$Task4ReportRoot = 'C:\Projects\IDIS\IDIS\.local_reports\slice100\acceptance-015'
$StrictNativeProcess = Join-Path $Worktree 'scripts\invoke_strict_native_process.ps1'
$StrictProcessIdentity = Join-Path $Worktree 'scripts\strict_process_identity.psm1'
$Python = 'C:\Projects\IDIS\IDIS\.venv\Scripts\python.exe'
Set-Location -LiteralPath $Worktree
$env:PYTHONPATH = "$Worktree\src"
$env:IDIS_STRICT_DOTENV_PATH = '<reviewed-task4-ingestion-secret-dotenv-path>'
Import-Module -Name $StrictProcessIdentity -Force

$ApiInitial = & $StrictNativeProcess `
  -FilePath $Python `
  -ArgumentList @(
    '-m',
    'idis.runtime.strict_launcher',
    'api',
    '--host',
    '127.0.0.1',
    '--port',
    '8000',
    '--pid-file',
    "$Task4ReportRoot\api.pid"
  ) `
  -WorkingDirectory $Worktree `
  -StandardOutputPath "$Task4ReportRoot\api.initial.stdout.log" `
  -StandardErrorPath "$Task4ReportRoot\api.initial.stderr.log" `
  -Background
if ($ApiInitial.SchemaVersion -ne 2 -or $ApiInitial.Mode -ne 'background') { throw 'TASK4_API_SPAWN_FAILED' }
$InitialIdentity = Wait-StrictServiceProcessIdentity `
  -LauncherProcessId $ApiInitial.LauncherProcessId `
  -ServicePidFilePath "$Task4ReportRoot\api.pid" `
  -ExpectedLauncherPath $Python
$InitialProcessReceipt = "$Task4ReportRoot\api.initial.process.json"
if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $InitialProcessReceipt) { throw 'TASK4_API_INITIAL_PROCESS_RECEIPT_NOT_FRESH' }
[IO.File]::WriteAllText(
  $InitialProcessReceipt,
  ($InitialIdentity | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 4 -Compress),
  [Text.UTF8Encoding]::new($false)
)

$InitialPortDeadline = [DateTime]::UtcNow.AddSeconds(60)
while (-not ([Net.NetworkInformation.IPGlobalProperties]::GetIPGlobalProperties().GetActiveTcpListeners() | Where-Object Port -EQ 8000)) {
  if (-not (Get-Process -Id $InitialIdentity.ServiceProcessId -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue)) { throw 'TASK4_API_INITIAL_SERVICE_PROCESS_EXITED' }
  if (-not (Get-Process -Id $InitialIdentity.LauncherProcessId -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue)) { throw 'TASK4_API_INITIAL_LAUNCHER_PROCESS_EXITED' }
  if ([DateTime]::UtcNow -ge $InitialPortDeadline) { throw 'TASK4_API_INITIAL_PORT_TIMEOUT' }
  Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 250
}

$Task4ApiAdmissionCode = @'
import hashlib, hmac, os
from uuid import UUID
import httpx
from idis.runtime.profile import load_strict_runtime_profile
def require(value, code):
    if not value:
        raise SystemExit(code)
p = load_strict_runtime_profile(command='gate')
key = p.values.get('IDIS_REAL_EXAMPLE_API_KEY', '')
expected = hashlib.sha256(p.base_profile_hmac.encode('ascii')).hexdigest()
require(bool(key), 'TASK4_API_AUTH_KEY_MISSING')
headers = {'X-IDIS-API-Key': key}
with httpx.Client(base_url='http://127.0.0.1:8000', timeout=10.0, trust_env=False) as c:
    health_response = c.get('/health', headers=headers)
    health = health_response.json()
    require(health_response.status_code == 200 and isinstance(health, dict), 'TASK4_API_HEALTH_INVALID')
    require(set(health) == {'status', 'time', 'version', 'boot_id'}, 'TASK4_API_HEALTH_INVALID')
    boot_id = UUID(str(health.get('boot_id')))
    require(health.get('status') == 'ok' and health.get('version') == '6.3' and boot_id.version == 4, 'TASK4_API_HEALTH_INVALID')
    readiness_response = c.get('/v1/strict-readiness', headers=headers)
    readiness = readiness_response.json()
    require(readiness_response.status_code == 200 and isinstance(readiness, dict), 'TASK4_API_STRICT_READINESS_INVALID')
    observed = readiness.get('profile_hmac_sha256')
    require(isinstance(observed, str) and hmac.compare_digest(observed, expected), 'TASK4_API_PROFILE_MISMATCH')
print('TASK4_API_AUTHENTICATED_STRICT_READINESS_GREEN')
'@
$InitialAdmission = & $StrictNativeProcess `
  -FilePath $Python `
  -ArgumentList @(
    '-c',
    $Task4ApiAdmissionCode
  ) `
  -WorkingDirectory $Worktree `
  -StandardOutputPath "$Task4ReportRoot\api.initial.admission.stdout.log" `
  -StandardErrorPath "$Task4ReportRoot\api.initial.admission.stderr.log"
if ($InitialAdmission.ExitCode -ne 0) { throw 'TASK4_API_INITIAL_ADMISSION_FAILED' }
Get-StrictServiceProcessIdentity `
  -LauncherProcessId $InitialIdentity.LauncherProcessId `
  -ServiceProcessId $InitialIdentity.ServiceProcessId `
  -ExpectedLauncherPath $Python | Out-Null
```

From a fresh PowerShell process, run the complete initial ingestion proof. Its foreground wrapper returns only after the gate exits zero and both capture streams are closed:

```powershell
Set-StrictMode -Version Latest
$ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'
$Worktree = 'C:\Projects\IDIS\IDIS-slice100'
$DataRoot = 'C:\Projects\IDIS\IDIS\real_example'
$Task4ReportRoot = 'C:\Projects\IDIS\IDIS\.local_reports\slice100\acceptance-015'
$StrictNativeProcess = Join-Path $Worktree 'scripts\invoke_strict_native_process.ps1'
$Python = 'C:\Projects\IDIS\IDIS\.venv\Scripts\python.exe'
Set-Location -LiteralPath $Worktree
$env:PYTHONPATH = "$Worktree\src"
$env:IDIS_STRICT_DOTENV_PATH = '<reviewed-task4-ingestion-secret-dotenv-path>'

$InitialGate = & $StrictNativeProcess `
  -FilePath $Python `
  -ArgumentList @(
    '-m',
    'idis.runtime.strict_launcher',
    'gate',
    '--root', $DataRoot,
    '--api-base-url', 'http://127.0.0.1:8000',
    '--api-key-env', 'IDIS_REAL_EXAMPLE_API_KEY',
```
