```powershell
    '--no-secret-output'
  ) `
  -WorkingDirectory $Worktree `
  -StandardOutputPath "$Task4ReportRoot\gate.initial.stdout.log" `
  -StandardErrorPath "$Task4ReportRoot\gate.initial.stderr.log"
if ($InitialGate.ExitCode -ne 0) { throw 'TASK4_INITIAL_INGESTION_PROOF_FAILED' }
```

Capture the first safe boot hash from the persisted proof, revalidate the recorded service relationship, force-terminate the service PID, prove both recorded processes dead and the port closed, then make the second API boot explicit with fresh capture paths:

```powershell
Set-StrictMode -Version Latest
$ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'
$Worktree = 'C:\Projects\IDIS\IDIS-slice100'
$Task4ReportRoot = 'C:\Projects\IDIS\IDIS\.local_reports\slice100\acceptance-015'
$StrictNativeProcess = Join-Path $Worktree 'scripts\invoke_strict_native_process.ps1'
$StrictProcessIdentity = Join-Path $Worktree 'scripts\strict_process_identity.psm1'
$Python = 'C:\Projects\IDIS\IDIS\.venv\Scripts\python.exe'
Set-Location -LiteralPath $Worktree
$env:PYTHONPATH = "$Worktree\src"
$env:IDIS_STRICT_DOTENV_PATH = '<reviewed-task4-ingestion-secret-dotenv-path>'
Import-Module -Name $StrictProcessIdentity -Force

$InitialIdentity = Get-Content -LiteralPath "$Task4ReportRoot\api.initial.process.json" -Raw | ConvertFrom-Json
$InitialKillIdentity = Wait-StrictServiceProcessIdentity `
  -LauncherProcessId $InitialIdentity.LauncherProcessId `
  -ServicePidFilePath "$Task4ReportRoot\api.pid" `
  -ExpectedLauncherPath $Python
if (
  $InitialKillIdentity.ServiceProcessId -ne $InitialIdentity.ServiceProcessId `
  -or $InitialKillIdentity.LauncherProcessId -ne $InitialIdentity.LauncherProcessId `
  -or $InitialKillIdentity.ServiceExecutableSha256 -ne $InitialIdentity.ServiceExecutableSha256 `
  -or $InitialKillIdentity.LauncherExecutableSha256 -ne $InitialIdentity.LauncherExecutableSha256
) { throw 'TASK4_API_INITIAL_IDENTITY_CHANGED_BEFORE_KILL' }
Stop-Process -Id $InitialIdentity.ServiceProcessId -Force
$InitialShutdownDeadline = [DateTime]::UtcNow.AddSeconds(60)
do {
  $InitialServiceAlive = [bool](Get-Process -Id $InitialIdentity.ServiceProcessId -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue)
  $InitialLauncherAlive = [bool](Get-Process -Id $InitialIdentity.LauncherProcessId -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue)
  $InitialPortOpen = [bool]([Net.NetworkInformation.IPGlobalProperties]::GetIPGlobalProperties().GetActiveTcpListeners() | Where-Object Port -EQ 8000)
  if (-not $InitialServiceAlive -and -not $InitialLauncherAlive -and -not $InitialPortOpen) { break }
  Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 250
} while ([DateTime]::UtcNow -lt $InitialShutdownDeadline)
if ($InitialServiceAlive) { throw 'TASK4_API_INITIAL_SERVICE_PROCESS_STILL_RUNNING' }
if ($InitialLauncherAlive) { throw 'TASK4_API_INITIAL_LAUNCHER_PROCESS_STILL_RUNNING' }
if ($InitialPortOpen) { throw 'TASK4_API_PORT_STILL_LISTENING' }

$ApiRestart = & $StrictNativeProcess `
  -FilePath $Python `
  -ArgumentList @(
    '-m',
    'idis.runtime.strict_launcher',
    'api',
    '--host',
    '127.0.0.1',
    '--port',
    '8000',
    '--pid-file',
    "$Task4ReportRoot\api.pid"
  ) `
  -WorkingDirectory $Worktree `
  -StandardOutputPath "$Task4ReportRoot\api.restart.stdout.log" `
  -StandardErrorPath "$Task4ReportRoot\api.restart.stderr.log" `
  -Background
if ($ApiRestart.SchemaVersion -ne 2 -or $ApiRestart.Mode -ne 'background') { throw 'TASK4_API_RESTART_SPAWN_FAILED' }
$RestartIdentity = Wait-StrictServiceProcessIdentity `
  -LauncherProcessId $ApiRestart.LauncherProcessId `
  -ServicePidFilePath "$Task4ReportRoot\api.pid" `
  -ExpectedLauncherPath $Python
if ($RestartIdentity.ServiceProcessId -eq $InitialIdentity.ServiceProcessId) { throw 'TASK4_API_RESTART_SERVICE_PID_REUSED' }
$RestartProcessReceipt = "$Task4ReportRoot\api.restart.process.json"
if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $RestartProcessReceipt) { throw 'TASK4_API_RESTART_PROCESS_RECEIPT_NOT_FRESH' }
[IO.File]::WriteAllText(
  $RestartProcessReceipt,
  ($RestartIdentity | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 4 -Compress),
  [Text.UTF8Encoding]::new($false)
)

$RestartPortDeadline = [DateTime]::UtcNow.AddSeconds(60)
while (-not ([Net.NetworkInformation.IPGlobalProperties]::GetIPGlobalProperties().GetActiveTcpListeners() | Where-Object Port -EQ 8000)) {
  if (-not (Get-Process -Id $RestartIdentity.ServiceProcessId -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue)) { throw 'TASK4_API_RESTART_SERVICE_PROCESS_EXITED' }
  if (-not (Get-Process -Id $RestartIdentity.LauncherProcessId -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue)) { throw 'TASK4_API_RESTART_LAUNCHER_PROCESS_EXITED' }
  if ([DateTime]::UtcNow -ge $RestartPortDeadline) { throw 'TASK4_API_RESTART_PORT_TIMEOUT' }
  Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 250
}

$Task4ApiAdmissionCode = @'
import hashlib, hmac, os
from uuid import UUID
import httpx
from idis.runtime.profile import load_strict_runtime_profile
def require(value, code):
    if not value:
        raise SystemExit(code)
p = load_strict_runtime_profile(command='gate')
key = p.values.get('IDIS_REAL_EXAMPLE_API_KEY', '')
expected = hashlib.sha256(p.base_profile_hmac.encode('ascii')).hexdigest()
require(bool(key), 'TASK4_API_AUTH_KEY_MISSING')
headers = {'X-IDIS-API-Key': key}
with httpx.Client(base_url='http://127.0.0.1:8000', timeout=10.0, trust_env=False) as c:
    health_response = c.get('/health', headers=headers)
    health = health_response.json()
    require(health_response.status_code == 200 and isinstance(health, dict), 'TASK4_API_HEALTH_INVALID')
    require(set(health) == {'status', 'time', 'version', 'boot_id'}, 'TASK4_API_HEALTH_INVALID')
    boot_id = UUID(str(health.get('boot_id')))
    require(health.get('status') == 'ok' and health.get('version') == '6.3' and boot_id.version == 4, 'TASK4_API_HEALTH_INVALID')
    readiness_response = c.get('/v1/strict-readiness', headers=headers)
    readiness = readiness_response.json()
    require(readiness_response.status_code == 200 and isinstance(readiness, dict), 'TASK4_API_STRICT_READINESS_INVALID')
    observed = readiness.get('profile_hmac_sha256')
    require(isinstance(observed, str) and hmac.compare_digest(observed, expected), 'TASK4_API_PROFILE_MISMATCH')
print('TASK4_API_AUTHENTICATED_STRICT_READINESS_GREEN')
'@
$RestartAdmission = & $StrictNativeProcess `
  -FilePath $Python `
  -ArgumentList @(
    '-c',
    $Task4ApiAdmissionCode
  ) `
  -WorkingDirectory $Worktree `
  -StandardOutputPath "$Task4ReportRoot\api.restart.admission.stdout.log" `
  -StandardErrorPath "$Task4ReportRoot\api.restart.admission.stderr.log"
if ($RestartAdmission.ExitCode -ne 0) { throw 'TASK4_API_RESTART_ADMISSION_FAILED' }
Get-StrictServiceProcessIdentity `
  -LauncherProcessId $RestartIdentity.LauncherProcessId `
  -ServiceProcessId $RestartIdentity.ServiceProcessId `
  -ExpectedLauncherPath $Python | Out-Null
```

After strict admission proves a distinct boot, run restart verification. It must perform zero upload POSTs, reproduce both authenticated downloads and all 267 rows, and pass the complete post-restart isolation matrix before persisting the restart-verified receipt:

```powershell
Set-StrictMode -Version Latest
$ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'
$Worktree = 'C:\Projects\IDIS\IDIS-slice100'
$DataRoot = 'C:\Projects\IDIS\IDIS\real_example'
$Task4ReportRoot = 'C:\Projects\IDIS\IDIS\.local_reports\slice100\acceptance-015'
$StrictNativeProcess = Join-Path $Worktree 'scripts\invoke_strict_native_process.ps1'
$Python = 'C:\Projects\IDIS\IDIS\.venv\Scripts\python.exe'
Set-Location -LiteralPath $Worktree
$env:PYTHONPATH = "$Worktree\src"
$env:IDIS_STRICT_DOTENV_PATH = '<reviewed-task4-ingestion-secret-dotenv-path>'

$RestartGate = & $StrictNativeProcess `
  -FilePath $Python `
  -ArgumentList @(
    '-m',
    'idis.runtime.strict_launcher',
    'gate',
    '--root', $DataRoot,
    '--api-base-url', 'http://127.0.0.1:8000',
    '--api-key-env', 'IDIS_REAL_EXAMPLE_API_KEY',
    '--canary-api-key-env', 'IDIS_ISOLATION_CANARY_API_KEY',
    '--require-full-live',
    '--verify-only',
    '--verify-restart-survival',
    '--verify-tenant-isolation',
    '--company-mode', 'isolated',
    '--expected-company-count', '2',
    '--expected-file-counts', '58,209',
    '--expected-total-files', '267',
    '--safe-report-dir', $Task4ReportRoot,
    '--resume-ledger', "$Task4ReportRoot\prepare-ledger.jsonl",
    '--no-secret-output'
  ) `
  -WorkingDirectory $Worktree `
  -StandardOutputPath "$Task4ReportRoot\gate.restart.stdout.log" `
  -StandardErrorPath "$Task4ReportRoot\gate.restart.stderr.log"
if ($RestartGate.ExitCode -ne 0) { throw 'TASK4_RESTART_INGESTION_PROOF_FAILED' }
```

After the restart receipt is durable, revalidate the second service relationship immediately before the final forced stop. Evidence sealing is forbidden until both recorded processes are dead and port 8000 is released:

```powershell
Set-StrictMode -Version Latest
$ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'
$Worktree = 'C:\Projects\IDIS\IDIS-slice100'
$Task4ReportRoot = 'C:\Projects\IDIS\IDIS\.local_reports\slice100\acceptance-015'
$StrictProcessIdentity = Join-Path $Worktree 'scripts\strict_process_identity.psm1'
$Python = 'C:\Projects\IDIS\IDIS\.venv\Scripts\python.exe'
Import-Module -Name $StrictProcessIdentity -Force

$RestartIdentity = Get-Content -LiteralPath "$Task4ReportRoot\api.restart.process.json" -Raw | ConvertFrom-Json
$RestartKillIdentity = Wait-StrictServiceProcessIdentity `
  -LauncherProcessId $RestartIdentity.LauncherProcessId `
  -ServicePidFilePath "$Task4ReportRoot\api.pid" `
  -ExpectedLauncherPath $Python
if (
  $RestartKillIdentity.ServiceProcessId -ne $RestartIdentity.ServiceProcessId `
  -or $RestartKillIdentity.LauncherProcessId -ne $RestartIdentity.LauncherProcessId `
  -or $RestartKillIdentity.ServiceExecutableSha256 -ne $RestartIdentity.ServiceExecutableSha256 `
  -or $RestartKillIdentity.LauncherExecutableSha256 -ne $RestartIdentity.LauncherExecutableSha256
) { throw 'TASK4_API_RESTART_IDENTITY_CHANGED_BEFORE_KILL' }
Stop-Process -Id $RestartIdentity.ServiceProcessId -Force
$RestartShutdownDeadline = [DateTime]::UtcNow.AddSeconds(60)
do {
  $RestartServiceAlive = [bool](Get-Process -Id $RestartIdentity.ServiceProcessId -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue)
  $RestartLauncherAlive = [bool](Get-Process -Id $RestartIdentity.LauncherProcessId -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue)
  $RestartPortOpen = [bool]([Net.NetworkInformation.IPGlobalProperties]::GetIPGlobalProperties().GetActiveTcpListeners() | Where-Object Port -EQ 8000)
  if (-not $RestartServiceAlive -and -not $RestartLauncherAlive -and -not $RestartPortOpen) { break }
  Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 250
} while ([DateTime]::UtcNow -lt $RestartShutdownDeadline)
if ($RestartServiceAlive) { throw 'TASK4_API_RESTART_SERVICE_PROCESS_STILL_RUNNING' }
if ($RestartLauncherAlive) { throw 'TASK4_API_RESTART_LAUNCHER_PROCESS_STILL_RUNNING' }
if ($RestartPortOpen) { throw 'TASK4_API_PORT_STILL_LISTENING' }
```

For Task 4, `--preflight-only` names the ingestion-terminal preparation mode only; it does not run the later semantic/provider preflight or create a `may_proceed=true` proof. Direct invocation of the gate module or legacy script remains forbidden. Every native process above is executed by the code-hash-bound wrapper, and stderr content alone can neither pass nor fail a phase.

### 16.1 Safe shell setup; then stop at Gate A

```powershell
$Worktree = 'C:\Projects\IDIS\IDIS-slice100'
$DataRoot = 'C:\Projects\IDIS\IDIS\real_example'
$ReportRoot = 'C:\Projects\IDIS\IDIS\.local_reports\slice100'
$StrictNativeProcess = Join-Path $Worktree 'scripts\invoke_strict_native_process.ps1'
$StrictProcessIdentity = Join-Path $Worktree 'scripts\strict_process_identity.psm1'
$Python = 'C:\Projects\IDIS\IDIS\.venv\Scripts\python.exe'
Set-Location -LiteralPath $Worktree
$env:PYTHONPATH = "$Worktree\src"
$env:IDIS_STRICT_DOTENV_PATH = '<approved-secret-dotenv-path>'
Import-Module -Name $StrictProcessIdentity -Force
```

This sets paths only. It does not parse the dotenv, connect to a service, migrate, start a server, or run a probe. **Stop here and obtain explicit Gate A approval.**

The pre-promotion profile is explicitly `IDIS_RUNTIME_PROFILE_GENERATION=bootstrap-v1`; it may keep `IDIS_REQUIRE_PROMOTED_PROMPTS=0` only for governance evaluation/promotion commands, and strict run creation is disabled in that generation. The final acceptance profile must be `acceptance-v1` with `IDIS_REQUIRE_PROMOTED_PROMPTS=1`.

### 16.2 After Gate A: load one profile, migrate, import governance, and probe

The new strict launcher loads and validates the profile before importing Alembic or IDIS runtime modules. It propagates the same effective config and persists only its HMAC.

```powershell

$Governance = & $StrictNativeProcess `

  ) `

  -StandardErrorPath "$ReportRoot\governance.stderr.log"

$LivePreflight = & $StrictNativeProcess `

    '--safe-report-dir', $ReportRoot,
```

Any failure stops. After evaluation thresholds and the required genuine human approvals exist, promote through the same durable governance service:

```powershell
$PromptPromotion = & $StrictNativeProcess `
  -FilePath $Python `
  -ArgumentList @(
    '-m',

```

Set `IDIS_REQUIRE_PROMOTED_PROMPTS=1` and `IDIS_RUNTIME_PROFILE_GENERATION=acceptance-v1` in the approved secret profile. The final base-profile HMAC is computed on the next launch; no pre-promotion proof is reusable. Final readiness revalidates bootstrap probe receipts against component-specific config hashes.

### 16.3 Start role-separated real API and worker

Terminal A (API only; launcher sets `IDIS_PROCESS_ROLE=api` and asserts no in-process worker):

```powershell
$Worktree = 'C:\Projects\IDIS\IDIS-slice100'
$ReportRoot = 'C:\Projects\IDIS\IDIS\.local_reports\slice100'
$StrictNativeProcess = Join-Path $Worktree 'scripts\invoke_strict_native_process.ps1'
$StrictProcessIdentity = Join-Path $Worktree 'scripts\strict_process_identity.psm1'
$Python = 'C:\Projects\IDIS\IDIS\.venv\Scripts\python.exe'
Set-Location -LiteralPath $Worktree
$env:PYTHONPATH = "$Worktree\src"
$env:IDIS_STRICT_DOTENV_PATH = '<approved-secret-dotenv-path>'
Import-Module -Name $StrictProcessIdentity -Force

$ApiInitial = & $StrictNativeProcess `
  -FilePath $Python `
  -ArgumentList @(
    '-m',
    'idis.runtime.strict_launcher',
    'api',
    '--host', '127.0.0.1',
    '--port', '8000',
    '--pid-file', "$ReportRoot\api.pid"
  ) `
  -WorkingDirectory $Worktree `
  -StandardOutputPath "$ReportRoot\api.initial.stdout.log" `
  -StandardErrorPath "$ReportRoot\api.initial.stderr.log" `
  -Background
if ($ApiInitial.SchemaVersion -ne 2 -or $ApiInitial.Mode -ne 'background') { throw 'FULL_LIVE_API_INITIAL_SPAWN_FAILED' }
$ApiInitialIdentity = Wait-StrictServiceProcessIdentity `
  -LauncherProcessId $ApiInitial.LauncherProcessId `
  -ServicePidFilePath "$ReportRoot\api.pid" `
  -ExpectedLauncherPath $Python
$ApiInitialProcessReceipt = "$ReportRoot\api.initial.process.json"
if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $ApiInitialProcessReceipt) { throw 'FULL_LIVE_API_INITIAL_PROCESS_RECEIPT_NOT_FRESH' }
[IO.File]::WriteAllText(
  $ApiInitialProcessReceipt,
  ($ApiInitialIdentity | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 4 -Compress),
  [Text.UTF8Encoding]::new($false)
)
```

Terminal B (worker only; the watch interface is a Slice100 target):

The gate submits each company's bound one-use proof ID. The API atomically verifies and consumes it before creating the queued run. Company 2 is not queued until Company 1 passes.

### 16.6 Restart proof

After both companies pass, reload the two persisted process receipts, revalidate both live service relationships against their pid-files immediately before shutdown, force-terminate the service PIDs, and prove both services, both launchers, and the API port are dead:

```powershell
$StrictProcessIdentity = Join-Path $Worktree 'scripts\strict_process_identity.psm1'
Import-Module -Name $StrictProcessIdentity -Force
$ApiInitialIdentity = Get-Content -LiteralPath "$ReportRoot\api.initial.process.json" -Raw | ConvertFrom-Json
$WorkerInitialIdentity = Get-Content -LiteralPath "$ReportRoot\worker.initial.process.json" -Raw | ConvertFrom-Json
$ApiInitialKillIdentity = Wait-StrictServiceProcessIdentity `
  -LauncherProcessId $ApiInitialIdentity.LauncherProcessId `
  -ServicePidFilePath "$ReportRoot\api.pid" `
  -ExpectedLauncherPath $Python
$WorkerInitialKillIdentity = Wait-StrictServiceProcessIdentity `
  -LauncherProcessId $WorkerInitialIdentity.LauncherProcessId `
  -ServicePidFilePath "$ReportRoot\worker.pid" `
  -ExpectedLauncherPath $Python
if (
  $ApiInitialKillIdentity.ServiceProcessId -ne $ApiInitialIdentity.ServiceProcessId `
  -or $ApiInitialKillIdentity.LauncherProcessId -ne $ApiInitialIdentity.LauncherProcessId `
  -or $ApiInitialKillIdentity.ServiceExecutableSha256 -ne $ApiInitialIdentity.ServiceExecutableSha256
) { throw 'FULL_LIVE_API_INITIAL_IDENTITY_CHANGED_BEFORE_KILL' }
if (
  $WorkerInitialKillIdentity.ServiceProcessId -ne $WorkerInitialIdentity.ServiceProcessId `
  -or $WorkerInitialKillIdentity.LauncherProcessId -ne $WorkerInitialIdentity.LauncherProcessId `
  -or $WorkerInitialKillIdentity.ServiceExecutableSha256 -ne $WorkerInitialIdentity.ServiceExecutableSha256
) { throw 'FULL_LIVE_WORKER_INITIAL_IDENTITY_CHANGED_BEFORE_KILL' }
Stop-Process -Id $ApiInitialIdentity.ServiceProcessId -Force
Stop-Process -Id $WorkerInitialIdentity.ServiceProcessId -Force
$InitialRoleShutdownDeadline = [DateTime]::UtcNow.AddSeconds(60)
do {
  $ApiInitialServiceAlive = [bool](Get-Process -Id $ApiInitialIdentity.ServiceProcessId -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue)
  $ApiInitialLauncherAlive = [bool](Get-Process -Id $ApiInitialIdentity.LauncherProcessId -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue)
  $WorkerInitialServiceAlive = [bool](Get-Process -Id $WorkerInitialIdentity.ServiceProcessId -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue)
  $WorkerInitialLauncherAlive = [bool](Get-Process -Id $WorkerInitialIdentity.LauncherProcessId -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue)
  $InitialApiPortOpen = [bool]([Net.NetworkInformation.IPGlobalProperties]::GetIPGlobalProperties().GetActiveTcpListeners() | Where-Object Port -EQ 8000)
  if (-not $ApiInitialServiceAlive -and -not $ApiInitialLauncherAlive -and -not $WorkerInitialServiceAlive -and -not $WorkerInitialLauncherAlive -and -not $InitialApiPortOpen) { break }
  Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 250
} while ([DateTime]::UtcNow -lt $InitialRoleShutdownDeadline)
if ($ApiInitialServiceAlive) { throw 'FULL_LIVE_API_INITIAL_SERVICE_PROCESS_STILL_RUNNING' }
if ($ApiInitialLauncherAlive) { throw 'FULL_LIVE_API_INITIAL_LAUNCHER_PROCESS_STILL_RUNNING' }
if ($WorkerInitialServiceAlive) { throw 'FULL_LIVE_WORKER_INITIAL_SERVICE_PROCESS_STILL_RUNNING' }
if ($WorkerInitialLauncherAlive) { throw 'FULL_LIVE_WORKER_INITIAL_LAUNCHER_PROCESS_STILL_RUNNING' }
if ($InitialApiPortOpen) { throw 'FULL_LIVE_API_PORT_STILL_LISTENING' }
```

The launcher writes PID files atomically and rejects a stale file whose process is still alive. Restart both roles explicitly with the same final base-profile HMAC, PostgreSQL, Redis, Neo4j, and object-store configuration, but with fresh capture files:

```powershell
$StrictProcessIdentity = Join-Path $Worktree 'scripts\strict_process_identity.psm1'
Import-Module -Name $StrictProcessIdentity -Force
$ApiInitialIdentity = Get-Content -LiteralPath "$ReportRoot\api.initial.process.json" -Raw | ConvertFrom-Json
$WorkerInitialIdentity = Get-Content -LiteralPath "$ReportRoot\worker.initial.process.json" -Raw | ConvertFrom-Json
$ApiRestart = & $StrictNativeProcess `
  -FilePath $Python `
  -ArgumentList @(
    '-m',
    'idis.runtime.strict_launcher',
    'api',
    '--host', '127.0.0.1',
    '--port', '8000',
    '--pid-file', "$ReportRoot\api.pid"
  ) `
  -WorkingDirectory $Worktree `
  -StandardOutputPath "$ReportRoot\api.restart.stdout.log" `
  -StandardErrorPath "$ReportRoot\api.restart.stderr.log" `
  -Background
if ($ApiRestart.SchemaVersion -ne 2 -or $ApiRestart.Mode -ne 'background') { throw 'FULL_LIVE_API_RESTART_SPAWN_FAILED' }
$ApiRestartIdentity = Wait-StrictServiceProcessIdentity `
  -LauncherProcessId $ApiRestart.LauncherProcessId `
  -ServicePidFilePath "$ReportRoot\api.pid" `
  -ExpectedLauncherPath $Python
if ($ApiRestartIdentity.ServiceProcessId -eq $ApiInitialIdentity.ServiceProcessId) { throw 'API_RESTART_SERVICE_PID_REUSED' }
$ApiRestartProcessReceipt = "$ReportRoot\api.restart.process.json"
if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $ApiRestartProcessReceipt) { throw 'FULL_LIVE_API_RESTART_PROCESS_RECEIPT_NOT_FRESH' }
[IO.File]::WriteAllText(
  $ApiRestartProcessReceipt,
  ($ApiRestartIdentity | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 4 -Compress),
  [Text.UTF8Encoding]::new($false)
)

$WorkerRestart = & $StrictNativeProcess `
  -FilePath $Python `
  -ArgumentList @(
    '-m',
    'idis.runtime.strict_launcher',
    'worker',
    '--watch',
    '--poll-seconds', '2',
    '--pid-file', "$ReportRoot\worker.pid"
  ) `
  -WorkingDirectory $Worktree `
  -StandardOutputPath "$ReportRoot\worker.restart.stdout.log" `
  -StandardErrorPath "$ReportRoot\worker.restart.stderr.log" `
  -Background
if ($WorkerRestart.SchemaVersion -ne 2 -or $WorkerRestart.Mode -ne 'background') { throw 'FULL_LIVE_WORKER_RESTART_SPAWN_FAILED' }
$WorkerRestartIdentity = Wait-StrictServiceProcessIdentity `
  -LauncherProcessId $WorkerRestart.LauncherProcessId `
  -ServicePidFilePath "$ReportRoot\worker.pid" `
  -ExpectedLauncherPath $Python
if ($WorkerRestartIdentity.ServiceProcessId -eq $WorkerInitialIdentity.ServiceProcessId) { throw 'WORKER_RESTART_SERVICE_PID_REUSED' }
$WorkerRestartProcessReceipt = "$ReportRoot\worker.restart.process.json"
if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $WorkerRestartProcessReceipt) { throw 'FULL_LIVE_WORKER_RESTART_PROCESS_RECEIPT_NOT_FRESH' }
[IO.File]::WriteAllText(
  $WorkerRestartProcessReceipt,
  ($WorkerRestartIdentity | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 4 -Compress),
  [Text.UTF8Encoding]::new($false)
)
```

After the restart-verified receipt is durable, perform the same validated service-PID shutdown for the second API and worker boots. The evidence package is not sealable until both services, both launchers, and port 8000 are dead:

```powershell
$StrictProcessIdentity = Join-Path $Worktree 'scripts\strict_process_identity.psm1'
Import-Module -Name $StrictProcessIdentity -Force
$ApiRestartIdentity = Get-Content -LiteralPath "$ReportRoot\api.restart.process.json" -Raw | ConvertFrom-Json
$WorkerRestartIdentity = Get-Content -LiteralPath "$ReportRoot\worker.restart.process.json" -Raw | ConvertFrom-Json
$ApiRestartKillIdentity = Wait-StrictServiceProcessIdentity `
  -LauncherProcessId $ApiRestartIdentity.LauncherProcessId `
  -ServicePidFilePath "$ReportRoot\api.pid" `
  -ExpectedLauncherPath $Python
$WorkerRestartKillIdentity = Wait-StrictServiceProcessIdentity `
  -LauncherProcessId $WorkerRestartIdentity.LauncherProcessId `
  -ServicePidFilePath "$ReportRoot\worker.pid" `
  -ExpectedLauncherPath $Python
if (
  $ApiRestartKillIdentity.ServiceProcessId -ne $ApiRestartIdentity.ServiceProcessId `
  -or $ApiRestartKillIdentity.LauncherProcessId -ne $ApiRestartIdentity.LauncherProcessId `
  -or $ApiRestartKillIdentity.ServiceExecutableSha256 -ne $ApiRestartIdentity.ServiceExecutableSha256
) { throw 'FULL_LIVE_API_RESTART_IDENTITY_CHANGED_BEFORE_KILL' }
if (
  $WorkerRestartKillIdentity.ServiceProcessId -ne $WorkerRestartIdentity.ServiceProcessId `
  -or $WorkerRestartKillIdentity.LauncherProcessId -ne $WorkerRestartIdentity.LauncherProcessId `
  -or $WorkerRestartKillIdentity.ServiceExecutableSha256 -ne $WorkerRestartIdentity.ServiceExecutableSha256
) { throw 'FULL_LIVE_WORKER_RESTART_IDENTITY_CHANGED_BEFORE_KILL' }
Stop-Process -Id $ApiRestartIdentity.ServiceProcessId -Force
Stop-Process -Id $WorkerRestartIdentity.ServiceProcessId -Force
$RestartRoleShutdownDeadline = [DateTime]::UtcNow.AddSeconds(60)
do {
  $ApiRestartServiceAlive = [bool](Get-Process -Id $ApiRestartIdentity.ServiceProcessId -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue)
  $ApiRestartLauncherAlive = [bool](Get-Process -Id $ApiRestartIdentity.LauncherProcessId -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue)
  $WorkerRestartServiceAlive = [bool](Get-Process -Id $WorkerRestartIdentity.ServiceProcessId -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue)
  $WorkerRestartLauncherAlive = [bool](Get-Process -Id $WorkerRestartIdentity.LauncherProcessId -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue)
  $RestartApiPortOpen = [bool]([Net.NetworkInformation.IPGlobalProperties]::GetIPGlobalProperties().GetActiveTcpListeners() | Where-Object Port -EQ 8000)
  if (-not $ApiRestartServiceAlive -and -not $ApiRestartLauncherAlive -and -not $WorkerRestartServiceAlive -and -not $WorkerRestartLauncherAlive -and -not $RestartApiPortOpen) { break }
  Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 250
} while ([DateTime]::UtcNow -lt $RestartRoleShutdownDeadline)
if ($ApiRestartServiceAlive) { throw 'FULL_LIVE_API_RESTART_SERVICE_PROCESS_STILL_RUNNING' }
if ($ApiRestartLauncherAlive) { throw 'FULL_LIVE_API_RESTART_LAUNCHER_PROCESS_STILL_RUNNING' }
if ($WorkerRestartServiceAlive) { throw 'FULL_LIVE_WORKER_RESTART_SERVICE_PROCESS_STILL_RUNNING' }
if ($WorkerRestartLauncherAlive) { throw 'FULL_LIVE_WORKER_RESTART_LAUNCHER_PROCESS_STILL_RUNNING' }
if ($RestartApiPortOpen) { throw 'FULL_LIVE_API_PORT_STILL_LISTENING' }
```

## 17. Verification gate before any completion claim

Offline repository gate:

```powershell

python -c "import idis; print(idis.__file__)"

python scripts/forbidden_scan.py --repo-root .
git diff --check
```

Task-4 watermark amendment gate before `acceptance-004`:

- The new thematic RED suite fails only on the absent classifier/manifest-v2/receipt-v2/prescan/v4 behavior; the pre-amendment manual-v1 and Task-4 suites remain green.
- A read-only census rehashes all 267 files, exact 58/209 membership, 858,207,454 aggregate bytes, 223 PDFs, and all PDF pages; zero source stat/hash drift and zero scan errors are required.
- The immutable blocked `watermark-page-prescan-v1` receipt binds the policy, signed exemplar, complete candidate and unresolved sequences, deterministic reviewer sample set, per-company cap arithmetic, and its own hash. Document 00054 must be a candidate. A derived resolved receipt references that immutable hash and records exactly two confirmed watermark candidates plus eight confirmed substantive-image pages without editing the original. Any omitted or additional page stops the gate.
- Reviewer render directories and files are owner-only and read-only, contain only safe alias/page filenames, and stay outside the repository and `.local_reports`; every staged render is visually checked before handoff.
- The signed batch envelope re-verifies against the pinned public key and exactly matches the resolved prescan receipt, which in turn binds the sealed blocked prescan, before the `acceptance-004` profile is edited or migrated. Only after verification are the staged review renders deleted.

Pre-Gate-A mandatory offline gates (Task 15A):

- Disposable real-PostgreSQL migration upgrade → downgrade → upgrade, then all repository/RLS/cross-tenant/idempotency/audit/budget tests with `IDIS_REQUIRE_POSTGRES=1`. No skip counts as pass.
- Disposable Redis integration tests and persistent-filesystem restart tests.

- Independent runtime-path review, security/RLS/rights review, leakage review, and validation review before any commit or PR.

Post-Gate-A configured-service gates (Task 15B):

- Acceptance PostgreSQL/Alembic/RLS, Redis, persistent object-store, configured `NEO4J_DATABASE`, pgvector/OpenAI embedding, exact registry-derived 18-provider probes, LLM model probes, and real OCR/STT inference. Any provider-probe failure blocks Gate A.

- Final `acceptance-v1` profile with `IDIS_REQUIRE_PROMOTED_PROMPTS=1`; all component receipt/config hashes revalidated before private preparation can produce proof IDs.

## 18. Post-run verification per company

For each company, the verifier requires:

1. Exactly one isolated deal, run, source package, 28-row semantic ledger, and output package.
2. Exactly 15 durable product rows for:
   - screening snapshot PDF and DOCX;
   - IC memo PDF and DOCX;
   - truth dashboard JSON;
   - QA brief JSON;
   - executive summary JSON;
   - commercial diligence JSON;
   - financial diligence JSON;
   - risk register JSON;
   - Layer-2 IC challenge JSON;
   - evidence index JSON;
   - run summary JSON;
   - provenance appendix JSON;
   - product-bundle manifest JSON.
3. The manifest contains exactly the 14 non-manifest artifacts with expected type, format, MIME content type, byte size, SHA-256, and persisted URI reference.
4. Authenticated list/manifest/download succeeds for the owning tenant; streaming download hashes equal manifest hashes.
5. After API and worker restart, run/step/product rows and all downloads remain identical.
6. Tenant B and the other deal cannot list, infer, or download the run/artifacts; denial does not reveal existence.
7. Every factual artifact statement has a source claim/evidence/Sanad/calc/provider reference; every required producer-to-consumer receipt resolves.
8. Source-tree fingerprints match before/after.
9. Reports, logs, audit payloads, manifests, and public responses pass the secret/private-path/name/filename/object-key/raw-payload/prompt leakage scanner.
10. Task-4 source reconciliation preserves exact manual-v1, classifier-v2, batch-attested, runtime-unseen, per-company, and variant counts plus the ordered v4 receipt digest before and after restart. The auto-classifier cap passes by integer arithmetic, excluded pages have no spans, and any runtime-unseen row remains explicitly review-required until signed confirmation.

## 19. Slice100 completion wording

- Before Gate A: only “offline Slice100 implementation and verification completed” may be said.
- After Gate A but before Gate B: only “live probes and strict preflight passed” may be said.
- After one company: only that company's safe acceptance facts may be said.
- Only after both runs and all section 18 checks pass may the master-plan full-live claim be used.

a dedicated methodology-dashboard-row UUIDv5 authority, removes only the

`UUIDv5(NAMESPACE_URL, "https://idis.local/contracts/methodology-truth-dashboard-row-v2")`
= `6aef4159-c49a-560a-9f92-6c6089d7f7c3`. The legacy Slice-10 dashboard-row
namespace is not reusable because its frozen name tuple is claim/Sanad based,
not methodology-coverage based.

Because the existing append-frozen, unconsumed `fdd-formula-policy-v1` bytes
contain the cyclic row-payload recipe, they remain immutable and unconsumed;
the corrected recipe is `fdd-formula-policy-v2`. Every policy-bearing identity,

The held v22 artifact, fixture, and contract identifiers above are permanently
rejected and join the void blocklist. They may never be fixture-published,
migration-consumed, or runtime-selected. Regeneration and the disposable
application-role PostgreSQL census are authorized; formula/runtime code,
migration `0039`, commits, and live calls remain forbidden.

The generation-time identity guard is artifact-wide. It derives one graph
from every declared UUID name tuple and every declared hash/digest preimage,
then rejects duplicate producers, unresolved governed references, self-edges,
or any directed cycle. Dashboard tests are regression vectors only; the
admission algorithm may not depend on a dashboard-specific name or location.
Graph nodes are qualified by artifact, definition/runtime layer, scope, output
kind, symbol, and source pointer; an exact source-pointer manifest and closed
canonical recipe grammar fail on new or ambiguous shapes. The v23 fixture and
disposable PostgreSQL census both bind the same ordered graph digest and exact
node/edge/leaf/source counts. Graph admission occurs before any strict-profile
load or disposable database creation.

Canonical publication is an atomic gate-completion protocol, not a false
claim that four already-materialized files were created in one operation. The
exact policy-v2 fixture, artifact, and reviewer-map binding are immutable
pre-admitted prerequisites and are verified before any write. The publisher
then links only the v23 fixture followed by the v23 artifact, whose link is the
sole commit point. Its receipt reports `preexisting_verified` separately from
`created_publication_order`; missing, partial, byte-different, aliased, or
non-regular prerequisite bytes fail before any v23 mutation. Fresh disposable
targets continue to prove the complete four-file ordering and exact final-byte
binding without rewriting canonical append-frozen files.

historical row itself is not rewritten. The corrected candidate is
`full-strict-v24`.

V24 admission has five structural conditions:

1. **Frozen-v21 provenance.** V24 is derived only from frozen v21 artifact
   SHA-256
   `2eb504ab750e9c3c96a5eeada13b22aad36c8772369c21e65d0e93705ff9cffd`

   v22/v23 bytes cannot be derivation parents.
2. **General identity/hash graph.** Generation enumerates every UUID name
   tuple and every hash/digest preimage in the complete artifact and bound
   policy, then rejects unresolved producers, self-edges, or any cycle. The
   four non-dashboard RED vectors are permanent. Audit clarification: the

   guard-bypass mutation** (`step_id` and `source_lineage_object_id`, plus an
   independent synthetic-node vector), not a cycle present in unmodified v23
   draft bytes. The defect was that the purported general guard did not
   reject the injected cycle.
3. **Independent fixture authority.** Expected component and final hashes are
   recomputed by an independent verifier that does not import production
   materializer derivation code. Hand-written golden vectors cover the full
   dashboard chain: row UUID, projection hash, payload hash, native hashes,
   and final generic output UUID.
4. **SQL/recipe derived equality.** Executable dashboard SQL projection and
   preimage references are derived-equal to frozen policy-v2 recipes at
   generation. Any reference to a superseded recipe field fails admission;
   SQL and contract recipes cannot drift independently.
5. **Approved-semantics conservation.** One literal conservation manifest
   binds strict period ordering, positive-magnitude enforcement,
   source-normalization preservation, the exact erasure restoration tuple,
   the 100/100 dashboard guard, the acyclic identity contract, and policy-v2
   binding to their implementing artifact bytes. A missing or drifted entry
   fails admission with the semantic name.

Policy v2 remains unaffected, frozen, and green. The stop boundary is stricter
than the earlier v23 preparation language: no PostgreSQL census, migration
`0039`, formula/runtime implementation, commit, push, PR, durable application
mutation, or live call occurs before v24 returns with identifiers and the

### 1.76 Standing Delegation v2 (verbatim)

**Standing Delegation v2 — effective now through Task 15A, recorded in the master plan verbatim**

**Tier 1 — proceed without stopping. Log every use as a numbered decision record citing the precedent.**

**Version advancement after a rejected or defective candidate.** Void it, blocklist all rendered identifiers, advance the numeric version, derive from the last-good frozen parent plus the approved delta set. This is settled precedent (v19, v22, v23) and no longer needs a ruling.
**Mechanical and closure-only contract regenerations** — executability grants, admission-literal counts, harness SQL corrections — provided the semantic-vector set is byte-identical to the last reviewer-approved set, and the conservation census, general graph guard, and independent verifier all pass. You may freeze, census, and publish the result INACTIVE without waiting for me. The three automated guards you built are exactly what makes this delegable.
**Evidence-strictening corrections**: seed determinism, posture attestation, FK closure, teardown safety, timestamp redaction — anything that makes proofs harder to pass, never easier.
**Environmental recovery** of existing reviewed services: restarts, fresh disposable namespaces, sealing failed attempts. Never a new service, config change, or credential.
**Production-composition rewires that make production match an already-approved contract**, RED-first — closing a gap between approved capability and production reachability is execution of a ruling, not a new one.
**Collection-boundary changes** only with the established guard pattern: pinned inventory plus required-current-collected assertions.

**Tier 2 — still stops, no exceptions.**

Any change to an approved semantic vector: equations, predicates, bindings, dispositions, event and outcome sets, UUID namespaces or name tuples, hash preimages, edge rules, the step registry, RLS predicates.
Any authority expansion beyond minimal executability closure — anything that widens what a role can read or mutate past what the approved body strictly requires, proven minimal by census with no superset.
New tables, roles, environment variables, flags, or services.
Live, priced, or provider calls; prompt promotion; anything touching the real_example data.
Activation of any contract version, ever.
Task checkpoints and Gates A/B; commits still happen only at approved checkpoints.
Tie-breaker: genuine doubt means stop — but check the precedent log first, and if a recorded precedent covers the case exactly, apply it and log it.

**When a Tier-2 stop does occur**, package the ruling request with complete evidence and keep working on any authorized non-dependent item rather than idling — the way Track B has been running.

**Audit protocol:** every Tier-1 decision record is reviewed at the next task checkpoint. A decision I would have rejected reverts at your cost and narrows this delegation. The delegation is a loan against your track record; the audit is how it stays honest.

Tier-1 uses are recorded as immutable numbered entries beneath the relevant
task record. Each entry names the delegated category, cites its controlling
precedent, states the bounded change, and carries RED/GREEN evidence at the
next task checkpoint.

**Decision record T1-20260814-001 — constraint-valid census evidence.**
Category: evidence-strictening corrections. Precedents: the Task-7 v12
application-role masking correction, the v24 disposable-database ownership
token correction, and the approved v24 second-gate census boundary. The
bounded action is to replace replica-bypassed, placeholder-bearing Task-8
census setup with a deterministic, trigger-enabled production-composition
seed whose FK closure, posture, hashes, UUIDs, and application-observed
projection are stricter than the rejected harness. This record authorizes no
semantic-vector change, authority expansion, activation, migration, new
configuration, or live/priced call.

### 1.77 Task-8.2 requirement-model pre-slice ruling

The reviewer rejected the SQL-only census alternative after production
round-trip reproduced the exact policy/model mismatch:
`required_calculations.0.calc_type` is missing when a frozen policy-v2
four-field requirement is validated by the legacy two-field model. Census
execution remains held until the production path can produce and reload the
approved bytes without a lossy adapter. Full-strict-v24 does not advance:
`MethodologyQuestion` and `RequiredCalculation` are outside the 68 frozen v24
schema snapshots, while the extraction-task snapshot intentionally admits
open requirement objects.

The authorized pre-slice is bounded as follows:

1. Add a disjoint frozen
   `FddFormulaRequirement(formula_ordinal, formula_type, required, scope)`.
   Formula type is restricted to the ten frozen policy-v2 values and scope to
   the frozen closed scope set. Legacy versions accept only the existing
   two-field shape; the policy-v2 successor accepts only the four-field shape;
   mixed shapes and implicit coercion in either direction fail closed.
2. Prove every existing question payload, payload hash, trace digest, and
   rehydration byte unchanged against the real frozen Task-5 fixtures.
3. Make one named constructor the sole successor path. It verifies the frozen
   workbook hash, policy artifact SHA-256, policy SHA-256, and exact 79-row
   join before producing any question, then uses the existing
   import/freeze/selection repository path unchanged. Mismatch failures use
   stable, leakage-safe codes before persistence.
4. Prove production import -> freeze -> reload preserves all four requirement
   fields byte-for-byte.
5. Regenerate all 68 v24 schema snapshots byte-identically and prove the
   contract lock remains clean.

This pre-slice contains no formula evaluation, planning, dashboard behavior,
migration `0039`, activation, live/priced call, or commit. It is explicit
Tier-2 authorization rather than a use of Standing Delegation v2. Once green,
the application-role PostgreSQL census resumes in a fresh namespace with the
existing complete-FK, trigger-enabled, no-placeholder acceptance bar.

### 1.79 Frozen-v24 requirement-binding defect and census stop

The resumed production-shaped census exposed a contract defect before strict
profile loading or PostgreSQL access. Frozen authority
`dashboard-root-equation-v2` has expected SHA-256
`513ca92f3e3c1c610af724413a1342743fc09b781fcb6fe6d6481b50f8570708`;
its five decoded SQL statements have joined-body SHA-256
`6a6113a72f6e68011e7d6c992060faccf63201c3dd76153b61e5a56386cff34c`.
The body reads `item->>'calc_type'` three times and reads
`formula_type`, `formula_ordinal`, and `scope` zero times. The approved named
policy-v2 constructor produces exactly ten non-empty requirements, all
`required=true`, each with the exact four fields
`formula_ordinal|formula_type|required|scope`, and none with `calc_type`.
Consequently the frozen guard derives zero matching required calculations and
rejects every production-valid policy-v2 calculation set.

A permanent hermetic RED proves the mismatch using the named constructor and
also proves the signed census stops with
`TASK8_V24_DASHBOARD_REQUIREMENT_BINDING_INVALID` before profile, key, runner,
or database access. Adding `calc_type` to the four-field model, coercing
`formula_type` into a lossy legacy shape, or running direct SQL would violate
the explicit pre-slice ruling. Changing the guard's JSON identity binding or
predicate is an approved-semantic-vector change and therefore a Tier-2 stop.
The application-role census, signature, atomic publication, migration `0039`,
activation, and commit remain held. The corrected binding requires reviewer
authorization. Version disposition does not: Standing Delegation v2 applies
the settled v19/v22/v23 precedent once a rendered candidate is proven
defective.

the complete closed call-site inventory rather than an app-only subset.

The signed safe projection must bind the ledger count/hash and exact pre-GRANT

approved fifteen-field complete-execution-v2 root. Fixed and staged inputs must
be consumed from the same admitted regular-file handles; public runner/wrapper
APIs must expose stable codes without chained private exceptions and must not
swallow `BaseException`. These changes add no semantic vector, SQL predicate,

to GREEN implementation and two fresh independent reviews.

**Decision record T1-20260821-014 -- Linux failed-output quarantine without
pathname deletion.** Category: evidence tightening and failure-safe cleanup.
Portable POSIX APIs do not provide a conditional unlink-by-inode operation;
checking a pathname identity and then calling `unlink` necessarily leaves a
replacement window. The v27 signed census therefore supports only Windows and
Linux and rejects every other platform before output reservation, profile, or
database access. It must never pathname-unlink its failed output on Linux. It
first truncates and syncs the confirmed held
owned reservation descriptor to zero bytes regardless of current pathname
state. It then atomically moves a still-identity-matching output name through
the held parent directory descriptor to an unpredictable quarantine name,
reopens that name nonblocking, verifies it is the owned regular file, and
retains that owned tombstone. If the moved inode is not the owned
reservation, it is never deleted; it is restored to the requested name when
that can be done safely, otherwise retained and reported only through a stable
cleanup code. Unknown or prior tombstones are never auto-deleted. The requested
receipt path remains absent and can be retried. Windows may continue deletion
through the already-held file handle. This changes no receipt semantics,
schema, predicate, ACL, role, service, migration, activation, or candidate
identity and authorizes no database, profile, key, publication, or commit.

**Early authority audit, 2026-08-21.** The reviewer explicitly endorsed both
`T1-20260821-013` and `T1-20260821-014` as Tier-1 decisions. The endorsement
confirms the closed case-coordinate ledger as a census rather than an
instrumented sample, and confirms descriptor-first Linux quarantine plus
pre-access unsupported-platform rejection as the fail-closed cleanup model.
The remaining sequence is unchanged: close the Alembic execution-source and
public error-code guards RED-first, obtain fresh independent reviews, run the
signed sealed application-role census, and publish only an INACTIVE artifact
and fixture after every guard is green. Migration `0039`, activation, commit,
and live or priced operations remain held. The formal decision-log audit is
deferred to the Task 8.2 checkpoint with the signed receipt.

### 1.81 T1-20260814-002 root-aware persistence execution record

The delegated production-composition closure is complete. Six existing batch
writers now accept an optional keyword-only
`run_output_object_ids: Mapping[str, str] | None`: coverage, planned task, and
claim/evidence/Sanad/defect result-link persistence. Supplied maps require the
exact canonical native-row key set, unique canonical UUID roots, and no
missing or extra binding before INSERT. Omitted maps preserve the prior SQL
and NULL behavior. Only `run_output_object_id` is supplied; generated
class/kind columns and every schema, identifier recipe, hash preimage,
predicate, ACL, and transaction boundary remain unchanged.

Independent spec review found one Important replay gap: coverage conflict
reload and planned-task existing-graph reload initially failed to compare the
durable roots. Nine REDs reproduced same-map, NULL, missing, different, and
extra-root cases. The remediation performs exact durable comparison only when
the caller supplied a map; same-map replay passes, mismatches fail through the
existing stable coverage/task incomplete codes, and legacy callers execute no
new reload query.

Fresh implementation evidence is `208` focused plus adjacent Task-5/Task-6
cases green. Spec re-review is `PASS` with `79` fresh cases; independent code-
quality review is `APPROVED` with `24` seam and `58` adjacent cases green plus
one existing skip. Ruff, format, mypy, compileall, and diff checks are clean.
